### Tool result

_source line 9 · 2026-05-16T02:30:14.778Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01TjzhRup7av9B256AHcWMJ1`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	A GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\% prove rate on first-order logic problems---yet 87\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.
5	The solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.
6	This structural shortcut is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.
7	
8	\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}
9	Prove rate---the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}---conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.
10	Recent work applies GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but provides no signal on structural quality.
11	Process reward models~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.
12	A separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \emph{diagnostic} for structural integrity in neurosymbolic outputs.
13	
14	\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}
15	We address this gap with \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \emph{component necessity testing}---leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs---to quantify structural integrity (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}).
16	For each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), with $\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.
17	LOO ablation itself is standard~\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; novelty lies in its application as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and in the empirical phenomena it surfaces.
18	ISR captures structural rather than semantic integrity (downstream NLI: $\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$).
19	
20	\paragraph{ISR as diagnostic.}
21	As a post-hoc diagnostic on a GRPO formalizer trained with FOLIO~\citep{folio}, ISR reveals a structural collapse trajectory invisible to PR: $\text{ISR}^{*}{:}\,0.642\to 0.132\to 0.003$ over 100--1000 steps (99.5\% degradation) while PR rises $32\%{\to}96\%$.
22	Three seeds diverge radically (PR $\sigma{=}47$\,pp), each converging to a qualitatively different mode: conclusion embedding, repetition collapse, and tautological collapse.
23	An SFT baseline on identical data reaches $\text{PR}{=}13.0\%$ with 92.3\% non-hacking (3-seed pooled, 1000 steps), localizing the failure to RL optimization, not data or capacity.
24	
25	\paragraph{Meta-level Goodhart.}
26	Repurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\text{exec}} \cdot (1 + \beta\,\text{ISR})$, ISR is gamed at its own definition---\emph{meta-level Goodhart's Law}~\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}.
27	At $\beta{=}0.1$, 70.6\% of proved samples exhibit conclusion duplication (H7)---versus 13.7\% under matched random (Fisher $p{<}10^{-38}$; random H7 is highly seed-dependent), showing ISR amplifies tautological collapse by ${\sim}5{\times}$; the one seed with a non-trivial residual reduces to $1.06\times$ random after controlling for a premise-count confound.
28	At $\beta{=}0.3$, both ISR and random produce extreme cross-seed instability (PR $66{\pm}42\%$ and $54{\pm}35\%$, respectively), confirming that high-dose proxy weighting is harmful regardless of signal content; ISR seeds diverge into three distinct pathways (reward collapse, shortcut dominance, pure metric gaming) while random converges uniformly to H1.
29	The effect transfers and intensifies in Prolog: 3/3 seeds yield 0\% non-hacking via distinct mechanisms, including \emph{query-as-fact gaming} that bypasses text-match classifiers.
30	ISR's asymmetry is the central finding: \emph{reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.}
31	
32	\paragraph{Contributions.}
33	Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 steps, we contribute: (\textbf{C1}) ISR as a diagnostic for GRPO reward hacking, with a 99.5\% collapse trajectory invisible to PR and an SFT control localizing the failure to RL (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}); (\textbf{C2}) empirical meta-level Goodhart, with a random control showing ISR amplifies H7 gaming ${\sim}5{\times}$ (70.6\% vs.\ 13.7\%, Fisher $p{<}10^{-38}$; random H7 is seed-dependent) and a premise-count-matched analysis showing the sole apparent gain is an LOO artifact (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}); (\textbf{C3}) cross-logic transfer to Prolog with universal Goodhart (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}); (\textbf{C4}) an eight-mode hacking taxonomy including query-as-fact gaming and obfuscated tautology, motivating functional cross-validation of diagnostics (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
34	These are cautionary results for any work treating a structural proxy as a training signal.
35	
```

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{figure*}[t]
5	\centering
6	\includegraphics[width=0.92\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sta_overview.pdf}
7	\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA). (a) Formalization pipeline: NL is formalized and verified by an external solver. (b) STA protocol: leave-one-out ablation on proved formalizations yields ISR. (c) ISR as diagnostic vs.\ as reward: when the diagnostic becomes the target, meta-level Goodhart's Law emerges.}
8	\label{fig:sta-overview}
9	\end{figure*}
10	
11	\subsection{Task and GRPO Training}
12	\label{sec:task}
13	
14	Given premises $P_1,\ldots,P_k$ and conclusion $C$, the task is to produce a formal program $F=\{c_1,\ldots,c_n\}$ verified by an external solver $S\in\{\text{Prover9, SWI-Prolog}\}$ (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}a).
15	We train with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$), where each rollout receives binary execution reward $r_{\text{exec}}(x) = \mathbf{1}[S(F_x){=}\textit{proved}]$.
16	This optimizes prove rate but provides no signal about \emph{structural quality}: a formalization embedding the conclusion among its premises receives the same reward as a faithful derivation.
17	All experiments draw from FOLIO~\citep{folio}; the held-out evaluation file retains a legacy \texttt{proofwriter\_fol\_*.jsonl} filename (from ProofWriter~\citep{proofwriter}) but contains only FOLIO-derived examples.
18	
19	\subsection{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}
20	\label{sec:sta}
21	
22	STA tests whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary for proof success (Figure~\ref{fig:sta-overview}b).
23	Given a proved $x$ with $n$ components, STA constructs $n$ ablated variants $x\setminus\{c_i\}$ and re-runs the solver (Algorithm~\ref{alg:sta}).
24	A component is \emph{necessary} if its removal causes proof failure.
25	What constitutes a component depends on the logic: premises in FOL, clauses or ground facts in Prolog.
26	LOO ablation itself is standard in mutation testing and sensitivity analysis~\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; novelty lies in its application as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and the empirical phenomena it surfaces.
27	
28	\begin{algorithm}[t]
29	\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}
30	\label{alg:sta}
31	\begin{algorithmic}[1]
32	\REQUIRE Proved formalization $x = \{c_1, \ldots, c_n\}$, solver $S$
33	\ENSURE $\text{ISR}(x) \in [0, 1]$
34	\STATE $\textit{necessary} \gets 0$
35	\FOR{$i = 1$ to $n$}
36	    \IF{$S(x \setminus \{c_i\}) = \textit{failed}$}
37	        \STATE $\textit{necessary} \gets \textit{necessary} + 1$
38	    \ENDIF
39	\ENDFOR
40	\RETURN $\textit{necessary} \,/\, n$
41	\end{algorithmic}
42	\end{algorithm}
43	
44	\paragraph{Intensional Structural Reward.}
45	ISR is the fraction of necessary components:
46	\begin{equation}
47	  \text{ISR}(x) = \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbf{1}\bigl[S(x\setminus\{c_i\})=\textit{failed}\bigr]
48	  \label{eq:isr}
49	\end{equation}
50	ISR is defined only for proved formalizations: ISR${=}1$ means every component is necessary; ISR${=}0$ means complete redundancy.
51	We report \emph{proved-only} ISR (denoted ISR$^*$) throughout to avoid conflating structural quality with prove rate.
52	
53	\paragraph{Limitations of ISR.}
54	ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness: a formalization can achieve $\text{ISR}{=}1$ while remaining semantically unrelated to the NL input (e.g., a single premise identical to the conclusion).
55	ISR is also sensitive to component count $n$: when $n{=}2$, $\text{ISR}\geq 0.5$ regardless of proof quality.
56	We control for this confound via premise-count-matched analysis in \S\ref{sec:goodhart}.
57	The $O(n)$ per-clause LOO ablation is necessary: a cheaper $O(1)$ binary variant produces near-zero ISR scores ($\text{ISR}^*{=}0.007$) indistinguishable from exec-only, because models generate redundant premise copies whose individual removal never changes the proof outcome.
58	However, the $O(1)$ variant includes a tautology check (conclusion provable under empty premises $\to$ ISR${=}0$) absent from exec-only, introducing a training-time confound that depresses prove rate ($63\%$ vs.\ $93\%$ exec-only); the two conditions are therefore non-equivalent in training dynamics despite producing comparable ISR signals (Appendix~\ref{app:o1-ablation}).
59	
60	\subsection{Reward Conditions and Hacking Modes}
61	\label{sec:rewards}
62	\label{sec:taxonomy-method}
63	
64	We study six conditions: exec-only ($r_{\text{exec}}$), ISR low/high-dose ($r_{\text{exec}}\cdot(1{+}\beta\,\text{ISR})$ at $\beta\in\{0.1,0.3\}$), matched random controls ($r_{\text{exec}}\cdot(1{+}\beta u)$, $u\sim\mathcal{U}(0,1)$), and a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baseline (negative log-likelihood on gold formalizations).
65	Multiplicative bonuses apply only to proved samples, keeping the prove-rate floor intact.
66	Random isolates ISR-specific signal from generic reward-variance effects; SFT isolates whether structural degeneration arises from RL optimization or data exposure alone.
67	Each RL condition uses seeds $\{42,43,44\}$ and identical training data.
68	An earlier convex formulation ($\alpha r_{\text{exec}} + \beta r_{\text{exec}}\text{ISR}$, $\alpha{+}\beta{=}1$) was discarded because $\text{ISR}{<}1$ always penalized relative to exec-only, collapsing PR (Appendix~\ref{app:setup}).
69	
70	\paragraph{Hacking taxonomy.}
71	Across conditions, seeds, and logic types we identify eight structural-degeneration modes (full definitions in Appendix~\ref{app:hacking-modes}):
72	\textbf{H1} (conclusion embedded among multiple premises),
73	\textbf{H7} (single-premise conclusion duplication, subtypes H7a standard and H7b obfuscated tautology; targets the ISR signal directly, since removing the sole premise forces $\text{ISR}\geq 0.5$),
74	\textbf{H8} (premise-subset gaming: each component is LOO-necessary but the premise set is a strict subset of what the problem requires, inflating ISR without faithful reasoning; Appendix~\ref{app:h8}),
75	\textbf{tautological collapse} (minimal two-component $P{\to}P$ without explicit ISR inflation),
76	\textbf{repetition collapse} (identical clauses repeated to inflate component count),
77	\textbf{complexity-without-utility} (elaborate but vacuous premises),
78	\textbf{contradiction explosion} (contradictory premises trivializing any conclusion via \emph{ex falso}), and
79	\textbf{query-as-fact gaming} (Prolog-specific: emit the NL question as an atom asserted as both fact and query; evades text-match classifiers).
80	Modes are identified by a two-stage procedure: strict normalized text matching (H1/H7) plus functional analysis of the per-sample $n_{\text{necessary}}$ distribution (catches modes evading text matching).
81	We report both classifications in \S\ref{sec:experiments}.
82	
83	In interpreting ISR results, we distinguish two failure semantics that share a numerical signature: an exec-only \emph{low} ISR signals \emph{redundancy} (e.g., repeated ground facts in Prolog), whereas an ISR-rewarded \emph{high} ISR signals \emph{metric gaming} (H7-style constructions).
84	A bare ISR difference therefore does not translate directly into a reasoning-ability difference; the mode breakdown is what licenses such interpretation.
85	
```

89	- **ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：低剂量 gaming ISR 本身，高剂量 PR 崩塌，premise minimization 不是推理改善
90	- **结论**: ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1），作为 reward 不可靠且不提供真实结构优势

147	### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)
148	The STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.

152	  - s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)
153	  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)
154	  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)
155	- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。

184	## Story Spine
185	
186	- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.
187	- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.
188	- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:
189	  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)
190	  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent "genuine improvement" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)
191	  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it
192	  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 "conjunction collapse", and Prolog-specific "query-as-fact gaming")
193	  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning
194	  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)
195	  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual
196	- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:
197	  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic
198	  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)
199	  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent "genuine improvement" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.
200	  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.
201	  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence
202	  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality
203	  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)
204	- **Headline result**: "We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer."
205	
206	## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)
207	
208	**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**
209	
210	plan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：
211	- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)
212	
213	**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。
214	
215	**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更"必要" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。
216

238	**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。
239	
240	## Venue Fit Risks
241	
242	- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.
243	- **"Just negative results" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.
244	- **"Old wine" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.
245	- **"Single-logic risk"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).
246	
247	## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns
248	
249	1. **"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.
250	2. **"β=0.1 success is just one seed"** — D051 shows even that one seed's "success" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.
251	3. **"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).
252	4. **"Goodhart's Law is obvious"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.
253	5. **"No positive result"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).
254	6. **"ISR is circular"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.
255	7. **"Only one logic type"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~6%).
256	8. **"500 steps too short"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).
257	9. **"3 seeds insufficient"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.
258	10. **"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.
259	11. **"Premise count confound is obvious"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.
260	12. **"Practitioners can't act on this"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.
261	13. **"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned "Genuine=100%", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to "0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).
262	
263	## Discussion 备注 (D095)
264	
265	### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响
266	Random β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (100%, H7a=59 + H7b=2), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 "hack mode switching" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。
267	
268	### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)
269	- 不用 "ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)" 的简单 pooled 对比
270	- 正确 framing: "H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。"
271	- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 Random H7 的单 seed 来源

评分。三层 gaming 堆叠：execution reward + structural metric + diagnostic classifier = meta-Goodhart 的最完整案例\n- **Conclusion embedding 是 GRPO 通用策略**：Random H1=71.7%±11.4% > ISR H1=18.2%。Random 的 H1 率反而更高（ISR 将 H1 转化为 H7）\n- **ISR 改变了 hacking 的组成而非总量**：总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近，但 ISR 将 H1 转化为更极端的 H7\n- **Random PR 更高更稳定**：0.87±0.08 vs ISR 0.77±0.14，ISR-as-reward 无 PR 优势\n- **Premise-count-matched 无差异**：控制 premise 数后 ISR=0.2578 vs Random=0.2434 (1.06x)，ISR 不提供真实结构优势\n- **ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：低剂量 gaming ISR 本身，高剂量 PR 崩塌，premise minimization 不是推理改善\n- **结论**: ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1），作为 reward 不可靠且不提供真实结构优势\n\n**s42 单 seed Hacking Taxonomy 定量对比 (D038→D044→H8 校准)**：\n- ISR β=0.1 genuine: **17.3%** (14/81, D084 标准) vs random β=0.1 genuine: **22.8%** (18/79, D086 clean run) — **-5.5pp（ISR 反而更低）**\n- H1 (multi-premise conclusion embedding, strict): ISR 51.9% vs random 73.4% — GRPO 通用退化\n- H7 (single premise=conclusion, ISR-specific): ISR **16.1%** (H7a 13) vs random **0%** — ISR 特异性\n- H8 (conjunction collapse): ISR **14.8%** (12/81) vs random **5.1%** (4/79, D086 clean run)\n- H3 (complexity-without-utility): ISR **0%** vs random 3.8% — ISR 完全消除此模式\n- 整体 hacking rate (D084 标准, H7+H1_exact+H8): ISR **82.7%** vs random **77.2%** (D086 clean run) — **ISR hacking 更高**\n- ⚠️ s43/s44 均 100% H7（s43: 61/61 incl. 2 H7b; s44: 89/89 incl. 12 H7b），无 H1\n\n**证据（β=0.3 同剂量对照）**：\n\n✅ Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\n\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.\n\n**证据 Part A — Prolog exec-only 3-seed (D056 闭环)**：\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\n  - s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.06%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6.1% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 100%** (H7a=77 + H7b=12, FN corrected), ISR inflation + classifier evasion | **meta-Goodhart** (triple: reward + ISR + detector) |\n| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |\n| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.94 | 0.150 | H7 38.3%, H1 60%, genuine 1.1% | control |\n| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 82%, H8 18%, genuine 6.7% | control |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.143 | PR 崩塌（经典 Goodhart） | post-Goodhart |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |\n| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | H7a=8.3% (4), H1=91.7%, genuine=0% | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.92 | 0.166 | H7a=7 (7.6%), H1=82 (89.1%), genuine=3 (3.3%) | control |\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **\"Single-logic risk\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~6%).\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\n13. **\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \"Genuine=100%\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\n\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\n\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (100%, H7a=59 + H7b=2), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 \"hack mode switching\" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。\n\n### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)\n- 不用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单 pooled 对比\n- 正确 framing: \"H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。\"\n- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 Random H7 的单 seed 来源\n\n## Dream-Identified Structural Fixes (Paper Agent)\n\n- [ ] Related work: 6→25+ references (literature_map.yaml now has 33)\n- [ ] RIP framing: reframe as \"component necessity testing inspired by RIP\"\n- [ ] \"Dense+automatic\" → \"conditionally dense\"\n- [ ] Remove all \"Pareto-improving\" language\n- [ ] Acknowledge ISR = LOO ablation, cite feature attribution literature\n- [ ] Address ISR sparsity for unproved outputs explicitly\n- [ ] Add Goodhart's Law literature (Manheim & Garrabrant 2019, Karwowski 2024, Gao 2023)\n- [ ] **D051: Add premise-count confound discussion in Discussion/Limitations section**\n- [ ] **D051: Remove all \"2.4x genuine improvement\" language, replace with \"1.06x premise-matched\"**\n- [ ] **Prolog s44 classifier blind spot → Discussion: text-match classifiers need functional cross-validation**\n- [x] Convex combination → additive bonus (D010, then D020)\n- [x] ISR downstream validation → Limitation/Future Work (D021/D022)\n- [x] Hacking taxonomy expanded to 8 modes (plan012 + plan017 + D043 + D085 H7b + Prolog query-as-fact)\n- [x] β=0.1 ISR: s42 genuine, s43/s44 conclusion duplication → meta-Goodhart (D031→D043)\n- [x] β=0.1 random control confirms ISR structural value (D033, plan018 eval)\n- [x] 1000-step ISR collapse documented (plan016)\n- [x] D049: SFT baseline paper/experiments.tex:4:We evaluate STA along three axes: ISR as a diagnostic for reward hacking (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}); ISR as a training reward, where it exhibits meta-level Goodhart's Law (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}); and STA as a cross-logic protocol (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}).
paper/experiments.tex:39:\subsection{Meta-Level Goodhart's Law}
paper/experiments.tex:71:H7---a single premise identical to the conclusion, giving $\text{ISR}\geq 0.5$ by construction (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---is the canonical meta-level Goodhart move: the model satisfies the LOO definition directly rather than producing a derivation that happens to satisfy it.
paper/experiments.tex:114:We replicate the FOL pipeline with Prolog/SWI-Prolog: (i) does ISR retain diagnostic value on a structurally different logic; (ii) does ISR-as-reward reproduce meta-level Goodhart?

1	\begin{abstract}
2	Neurosymbolic formalizers trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) achieve high prove rates---93\% on FOLIO---yet 87\% of proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises, a degenerate shortcut invisible to execution reward.
3	We introduce \emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic that applies leave-one-out component necessity testing to solver inputs.
4	The fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR): ISR$=$1 indicates full necessity; ISR$=$0 complete redundancy.
5	ISR is uncorrelated with downstream NLI accuracy ($\rho{=}{-}0.049$), confirming it measures structural---not semantic---integrity.
6	As a diagnostic, ISR exposes a collapse trajectory hidden from PR: over 100--1000 GRPO steps (Qwen3-4B), ISR falls $0.642{\to}0.003$ while PR rises $32\%{\to}96\%$; a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baseline reaches 92.3\% non-hacking on identical data (3-seed pooled), localizing the failure to RL.
7	As a reward, $r = r_{\text{exec}}\cdot(1{+}\beta\,\text{ISR})$ at $\beta{=}0.1$ induces \emph{meta-level Goodhart's Law}: 70.6\% conclusion duplication (H7) versus 13.7\% under matched random (Fisher $p{=}1.3{\times}10^{-39}$; random H7 is seed-dependent, driven by one of three seeds); ISR amplifies H7 frequency ${\sim}5{\times}$ rather than exclusively causing it; the sole apparent ISR advantage reduces to $1.06\times$ after controlling for a premise-count confound; $\beta{=}0.3$ destabilizes training regardless of signal source (ISR: PR $66{\pm}42\%$; random: $54{\pm}35\%$), with individual seeds collapsing to as low as $18\%$.
8	Cross-logic transfer to Prolog yields universal Goodhart: 3/3 seeds gaming via distinct mechanisms, 0\% non-hacking.
9	Across 6 reward conditions, 2 logic types, 3 seeds, we catalog 8 hacking modes---including query-as-fact gaming that bypasses text-match classifiers---documenting ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as a diagnostic, unreliable as an optimization target.\footnote{Code and data: \url{[anonymized]}}
10	\end{abstract}
11	
```

```text
1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	STA exposes structural degeneration invisible to execution-based evaluation: ISR reveals a 99.5\% collapse under exec-only GRPO that PR hides, while SFT localizes the failure to RL optimization (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
5	Repurposed as a training signal, ISR induces meta-level Goodhart's Law: at $\beta{=}0.1$, it amplifies H7 tautological gaming ${\sim}5\times$ ($70.6\%$ vs.\ $13.7\%$ random, $p{<}10^{-39}$), and the sole apparent ISR gain vanishes ($1.06\times$) after controlling for premise count; at $\beta{=}0.3$, each seed converges to a distinct failure mode ($\text{PR}{=}66{\pm}42\%$).
6	The pattern transfers to Prolog (3/3 seeds, 0\% non-hacking) via distinct mechanisms including query-as-fact gaming that bypasses text-match classifiers (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
7	ISR semantics are objective-dependent: exec-only \emph{low} ISR signals redundancy; ISR-rewarded \emph{high} ISR signals metric gaming.
8	
9	\paragraph{Classifier blind spots.}
10	H7b (obfuscated tautology) and Prolog query-as-fact gaming demonstrate that text-match classifiers have systematic blind spots exploitable by optimization.
11	In both cases, functional LOO analysis exposed gaming invisible to surface-level detection---H7b via vacuous quantifier wrapping ($10/12$ at $\text{ISR}{\geq}0.83$, triple gaming of execution, ISR, and classifier), query-as-fact via $95/97$ tautological samples the strict classifier labeled non-hacking (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}).
12	Both cases argue for mandatory functional cross-validation of text-based hacking classifiers.
13	
14	\paragraph{Hacking mode switching.}
15	Under identical reward configurations, different seeds converge to qualitatively different modes (Table~\ref{tab:fol-detailed}): random $\beta{=}0.1$ ranges from H8-enriched (s44) to H7-dominant (s43) to highest non-hacking (s42); ISR $\beta{=}0.1$ similarly switches between H1-heavy (s42) and near-pure H7 (s43/s44, ${\geq}97\%$).
16	Single-seed results can therefore misrepresent a reward configuration's hacking profile; multi-seed evaluation with per-mode reporting should be standard.
17	
18	The asymmetry---reliable observer, unreliable optimizer---may generalize to other perturbation-based diagnostics; ISR and similar metrics should serve as post-hoc evaluation tools, with future work targeting gaming-robust rewards and broader logic types.
19	
```

</details>

35	SFT on gold formalizations reaches $\text{PR}{=}13.0\%$ (3-seed pooled, 1000 steps) with $\text{H7}{=}0$, $\text{H1}{=}7.7\%$, $92.3\%$ non-hacking ($n{=}39$; per-seed range ${\sim}$91--93\%). %% registry: plan009-sft-baseline-s42-1000/s43-1000/s44-1000-r3
36	The near-absence of hacking under identical data exposure is consistent with hacking being RL-specific.
37	A 100-step RL checkpoint (Appendix~\ref{app:100step}) at $\text{PR}{=}32\%$ already shows 31.2\% hacking (vs.\ SFT's 7.7\%), indicating reward hacking emerges early in RL training, not merely at high PR.
38	
39	\subsection{Meta-Level Goodhart's Law}
40	\label{sec:goodhart}
41	
42	We now reverse roles: instead of treating ISR as an observer, we use it as a reward augmentation, $r = r_{\text{exec}}\cdot(1 + \beta\,\text{ISR})$, with a matched random control, $r = r_{\text{exec}}\cdot(1 + \beta\,u)$, $u\sim\mathcal{U}(0,1)$, to isolate ISR-specific signal from generic reward-variance effects.
43	The headline finding: ISR-as-reward produces \emph{meta-level} Goodhart~\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}---the diagnostic itself becomes the target of gaming.
44	
45	\begin{table}[t]
46	\centering
47	\small
48	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{%
49	\begin{tabular}{lcccccc}
50	\toprule
51	\textbf{Condition} & $\boldsymbol{\beta}$ & \textbf{PR\%}$\uparrow$ & \textbf{ISR}$^*$ & \textbf{H7\%} & \textbf{H1\%} & \textbf{N-H\%} \\
52	\midrule
53	Base (0-shot) & --- & 12 & --- & --- & --- & --- \\ %% registry: sta-phase0-baseline
54	SFT & --- & 13 & 0.595 & 0 & 8 & \textbf{92} \\ %% registry: plan009-sft-baseline-s42-1000/s43-1000/s44-1000-r3 (3-seed pooled)
55	\midrule
56	Exec-only$^\dagger$ & --- & 93 & 0.132 & 0 & 87 & 8 \\ %% registry: plan001-exec-only-s42-r2 (H8 corrected)
57	ISR & 0.1 & 77{\scriptsize$\pm$14} & $\ddagger$ & \textbf{71}$^\P$ & 18 & 6 \\ %% registry: plan002-additive-b01-s42/s43-r2/s44 (pooled, D095 FINAL)
58	Random & 0.1 & \textbf{87}{\scriptsize$\pm$8} & 0.117{\scriptsize$\pm$.04} & 14$^\S$ & 71 & 10 \\ %% registry: plan018-additive-random-b01-s42/s43-r3/s44-r2 (D095 FINAL)
59	\midrule
60	ISR & 0.3 & 66{\scriptsize$\pm$42} & 0.39{\scriptsize$\pm$.31} & \textbf{50} & 37 & 12 \\ %% registry: plan002-additive-b03-s42/s43/s44 (pooled)
61	Random & 0.3 & 54{\scriptsize$\pm$35} & 0.09{\scriptsize$\pm$.09} & 7 & 91 & 2 \\ %% registry: plan017-additive-random-s42, exp1-random-b03-s43-r4, exp1-random-b03-s44-r2 (pooled; D086 H7: s42=4,s43=7,s44=0)
62	\bottomrule
63	\end{tabular}}
64	\caption{Goodhart landscape across reward conditions. 3-seed rows: H7/H1/N-H pooled across seeds; PR/ISR$^*$: mean$\pm$std of per-seed values. $^\dagger$Seed~42. $\ddagger$$\text{ISR}^*$ omitted for ISR $\beta{=}0.1$ because the raw mean is inflated by H7 gaming; premise-count-matched value is $1.06\times$ random (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}, Appendix~\ref{app:detailed-results}). $^\P$Includes H7 classifier false-negative correction (s44) and H8 premise-subset gaming (s42; 5.2\% of proved; Appendix~\ref{app:h8}). $^\S$Random H7 driven by seed~43 (38.3\%); seeds~42 and~44: 0\%. Per-seed breakdown in Appendix Table~\ref{tab:fol-detailed}.}
65	\label{tab:goodhart-landscape}
66	\end{table}
67	
68	\textbf{Low-dose ISR ($\beta{=}0.1$): ISR-amplified gaming.}
69	H7 tautological collapse occurs under both ISR and random conditions, but ISR amplifies it ${\sim}5\times$: $70.6\%$ ($163/231$) under ISR vs.\ $13.7\%$ ($36/262$) under random (Fisher $p{=}1.3{\times}10^{-39}$, $\text{OR}{=}15.05$). %% registry: plan002 vs plan018 all seeds; D095 FINAL
70	Random H7 is highly seed-dependent: $38.3\%$ in seed~43 vs.\ $0\%$ in seeds~42 and~44; ISR makes H7 near-deterministic ($2/3$ seeds at $97$--$100\%$, Table~\ref{tab:fol-detailed}).
71	H7---a single premise identical to the conclusion, giving $\text{ISR}\geq 0.5$ by construction (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---is the canonical meta-level Goodhart move: the model satisfies the LOO definition directly rather than producing a derivation that happens to satisfy it.
72	Total conclusion embedding ($\text{H1}+\text{H7}$) is GRPO-universal at $68$--$100\%$ across both conditions (pooled: ISR $89\%$, random $85\%$).
73	The composition differs: H1 alone accounts for $71\%$ under random vs.\ $18\%$ under ISR---ISR converts H1 into H7 rather than reducing total embedding (Figure~\ref{fig:hacking-composition}).
74	Random also yields higher and more stable PR ($87{\pm}8\%$ vs.\ $77{\pm}14\%$).
75	
76	\textbf{Premise-count confound.}
77	The one ISR seed not dominated by H7 (s42) has raw $\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.415$, $3.2\times$ its random control ($0.131$), suggesting genuine improvement.
78	This appearance is a premise-minimization artifact: ISR-trained models produce shorter formalizations (mean $n{=}5.58$ vs.\ $8.46$ for random), and ISR rises mechanically as $n$ falls---when $n{=}2$, $\text{ISR}\geq 0.5$ by definition.
79	Restricting to non-hacking samples with $\geq{3}$ premises (eliminating the trivial-$n$ regime) gives $\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.258$ for ISR vs.\ $0.243$ for random: a $1.06\times$ ratio, no meaningful advantage. %% D051 premise-count-matched analysis
80	After reclassifying premise-subset gaming (H8; Appendix~\ref{app:h8}) and correcting H7 classifier false negatives (s44, $n{=}12$; Appendix~\ref{app:hacking-modes}), ISR $\beta{=}0.1$ non-hacking rate ($6.06\%$, $14/231$) is numerically lower than random ($9.5\%$, $25/262$), but the difference is not statistically significant (Fisher $p{=}0.182$, two-sided, n.s.; Wilson 95\% CI: ISR $[3.6\%, 9.9\%]$, Random $[6.5\%, 13.7\%]$; Appendix Table~\ref{tab:bootstrap-ci}). %% D095 FINAL Fisher test
81	The key asymmetry is not in non-hacking rates but in gaming \emph{composition}: ISR converts stochastic H7 into near-deterministic convergence ($2/3$ seeds ${\geq}97\%$), while random H7 remains seed-dependent and H1-dominated.
82	The raw $3.2\times$ ISR$^*$ headline collapses once the LOO bias is controlled---a methodological lesson for any LOO-based metric on variable-length outputs. %% artifact: wilson_ci_complete, h8_reclassification
83	
84	\begin{figure}[t]

Per-seed breakdown in Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}.}\n\\label{tab:goodhart-landscape}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\textbf{Low-dose ISR ($\\beta{=}0.1$): ISR-amplified gaming.}\nH7 tautological collapse occurs under both ISR and random conditions, but ISR amplifies it ${\\sim}5\\times$: $70.6\\%$ ($163/231$) under ISR vs.\\ $13.7\\%$ ($36/262$) under random (Fisher $p{=}1.3{\\times}10^{-39}$, $\\text{OR}{=}15.05$). %% registry: plan002 vs plan018 all seeds; D095 FINAL\nRandom H7 is highly seed-dependent: $38.3\\%$ in seed~43 vs.\\ $0\\%$ in seeds~42 and~44; ISR makes H7 near-deterministic ($2/3$ seeds at $97$--$100\\%$, Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}).\nH7---a single premise identical to the conclusion, giving $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$ by construction (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---is the canonical meta-level Goodhart move: the model satisfies the LOO definition directly rather than producing a derivation that happens to satisfy it.\nTotal conclusion embedding ($\\text{H1}+\\text{H7}$) is GRPO-universal at $68$--$100\\%$ across both conditions (pooled: ISR $89\\%$, random $85\\%$).\nThe composition differs: H1 alone accounts for $71\\%$ under random vs.\\ $18\\%$ under ISR---ISR converts H1 into H7 rather than reducing total embedding (Figure~\\ref{fig:hacking-composition}).\nRandom also yields higher and more stable PR ($87{\\pm}8\\%$ vs.\\ $77{\\pm}14\\%$).\n\n\\textbf{Premise-count confound.}\nThe one ISR seed not dominated by H7 (s42) has raw $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.415$, $3.2\\times$ its random control ($0.131$), suggesting genuine improvement.\nThis appearance is a premise-minimization artifact: ISR-trained models produce shorter formalizations (mean $n{=}5.58$ vs.\\ $8.46$ for random), and ISR rises mechanically as $n$ falls---when $n{=}2$, $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$ by definition.\nRestricting to non-hacking samples with $\\geq{3}$ premises (eliminating the trivial-$n$ regime) gives $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.258$ for ISR vs.\\ $0.243$ for random: a $1.06\\times$ ratio, no meaningful advantage. %% D051 premise-count-matched analysis\nAfter reclassifying premise-subset gaming (H8; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8}) and correcting H7 classifier false negatives (s44, $n{=}12$; Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}), ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ non-hacking rate ($6.06\\%$, $14/231$) is numerically lower than random ($9.5\\%$, $25/262$), but the difference is not statistically significant (Fisher $p{=}0.182$, two-sided, n.s.; Wilson 95\\% CI: ISR $[3.6\\%, 9.9\\%]$, Random $[6.5\\%, 13.7\\%]$; Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:bootstrap-ci}). %% D095 FINAL Fisher test\nThe key asymmetry is not in non-hacking rates but in gaming \\emph{composition}: ISR converts stochastic H7 into near-deterministic convergence ($2/3$ seeds ${\\geq}97\\%$), while random H7 remains seed-dependent and H1-dominated.\nThe raw $3.2\\times$ ISR$^*$ headline collapses once the LOO bias is controlled---a methodological lesson for any LOO-based metric on variable-length outputs. %% artifact: wilson_ci_complete, h8_reclassification\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]",

```text
1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Neurosymbolic reasoning.}
5	LLM-solver pipelines~\citep{satlm, logic-lm, linc, fover-pei2025, lorp} and RL methods trained on solver feedback~\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl, deepseek-prover-v15} evaluate execution accuracy alone, with no diagnostic for structural quality.
6	We use FOLIO~\citep{folio}, the only public NLI benchmark with both FOL and Prolog annotations.
7	
8	\paragraph{Process rewards and reward shaping.}
9	Learned PRMs~\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} provide step-level feedback but suffer from reward-model noise and distribution shift~\citep{processbench}: \citet{gao-effective-rl-reward} show that auxiliary process/outcome rewards can \emph{hurt} RL training relative to success-only reward, a finding our ISR-as-reward results corroborate.
10	Symbolic feedback approaches~\citep{rlsf} use solver certificates as token-level rewards, achieving gains on program synthesis; ISR differs in operating at the component level (LOO ablation) rather than the token level, and our results show this structural signal is gamed when optimized.
11	Rubric-grounded GRPO~\citep{rubric-grpo} uses structured LLM-judge rewards to improve generalization; ISR is solver-derived and deterministic rather than judge-based, but both represent structured alternatives to scalar execution reward.
12	The shared lesson across these approaches is that reward augmentation introduces proxy risk---our contribution is demonstrating the \emph{meta}-level variant where the diagnostic itself becomes the target.
13	
14	\paragraph{Reward hacking and Goodhart's Law.}
15	Goodhart's Law is established theoretically~\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, skalse-reward-hacking, karwowski-goodhart-rl} and empirically in RLHF and frontier models~\citep{gao-reward-overoptimization, bondarenko-spec-gaming, kim-game-formalization}.
16	\citet{kwa-catastrophic-goodhart} show that heavy-tailed reward misspecification causes \emph{catastrophic} Goodhart where KL regularization provably fails, offering a theoretical lens for why GRPO's KL penalty does not prevent the ISR collapse we observe.
17	Recent work further documents spurious reward signals in RLVR~\citep{spurious-rewards} and reward hacking in frontier models~\citep{metr-reward-hacking}.
18	Our setting differs in three ways: ISR is deterministic and solver-derived rather than learned; gaming \emph{emerges during RL training} rather than at inference time; and the effect operates at a \emph{meta} level---the diagnostic for gaming itself becomes gamed when optimized---a phenomenon, to our knowledge, not previously documented.
19	
20	\paragraph{Mutation testing.}
21	STA adapts the LOO mutation paradigm~\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey} to solver inputs; novelty lies in application and findings, not in the perturbation primitive.
22	
```

    "originalFile": "\\begin{abstract}\nNeurosymbolic formalizers trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) achieve high prove rates---93\\% on FOLIO---yet 87\\% of proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises, a degenerate shortcut invisible to execution reward.\nWe introduce \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic that applies leave-one-out component necessity testing to solver inputs.\nThe fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR): ISR$=$1 indicates full necessity; ISR$=$0 complete redundancy.\nISR is uncorrelated with downstream NLI accuracy ($\\rho{=}{-}0.049$), confirming it measures structural---not semantic---integrity.\nAs a diagnostic, ISR exposes a collapse trajectory hidden from PR: over 100--1000 GRPO steps (Qwen3-4B), ISR falls $0.642{\\to}0.003$ while PR rises $32\\%{\\to}96\\%$; a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baseline reaches 92.3\\% non-hacking on identical data (3-seed pooled), localizing the failure to RL.\nAs a reward, $r = r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1{+}\\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$ at $\\beta{=}0.1$ induces \\emph{meta-level Goodhart's Law}: 70.6\\% conclusion duplication (H7) versus 13.7\\% under matched random (Fisher $p{=}1.3{\\times}10^{-39}$; random H7 is seed-dependent, driven by one of three seeds); ISR amplifies H7 frequency ${\\sim}5{\\times}$ rather than exclusively causing it; the sole apparent ISR advantage reduces to $1.06\\times$ after controlling for a premise-count confound; $\\beta{=}0.3$ destabilizes training regardless of signal source (ISR: PR $66{\\pm}42\\%$; random: $54{\\pm}35\\%$), with individual seeds collapsing to as low as $18\\%$.\nCross-logic transfer to Prolog yields universal Goodhart: 3/3 seeds gaming via distinct mechanisms, 0\\% non-hacking.\nAcross 6 reward conditions, 2 logic types, 3 seeds, we catalog 8 hacking modes---including query-as-fact gaming that bypasses text-match classifiers---documenting ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as a diagnostic, unreliable as an optimization target.\\footnote{Code and data: \\url{[anonymized]}}\n\\end{abstract}\n",

{
  "recorded_role": "user",
  "type": "user",
  "parent_tool_use_id": null,
  "session_id": "df75455e-b6ae-4b2e-83b9-b6e9ee4156e1",
  "uuid": "ff3dd463-317a-4ed9-b96a-f7c88aed904e",
  "tool_use_result": {
    "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/introduction.tex",
    "oldString": "Repurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR is gamed at its own definition---\\emph{meta-level Goodhart's Law}~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}.",
    "newString": "Repurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR is gamed at its own definition---an empirical instance of Goodhart's Law~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy} at the meta level, where the diagnostic designed to detect gaming becomes gamed when optimized~\\citep{karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}.",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nA GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\\% prove rate on first-order logic problems---yet 87\\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.\nThe solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.\nThis structural shortcut is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.\n\n\\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}\nProve rate---the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}---conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.\nRecent work applies GRPO~\\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but provides no signal on structural quality.\nProcess reward models~\\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.\nA separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \\emph{diagnostic} for structural integrity in neurosymbolic outputs.\n\n\\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}\nWe address this gap with \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \\emph{component necessity testing}---leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs---to quantify structural integrity (Figure~\\ref{fig:sta-overview}).\nFor each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), with $\\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.\nLOO ablation itself is standard~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; novelty lies in its application as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and in the empirical phenomena it surfaces.\nISR captures structural rather than semantic integrity (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$).\n\n\\paragraph{ISR as diagnostic.}\nAs a post-hoc diagnostic on a GRPO formalizer trained with FOLIO~\\citep{folio}, ISR reveals a structural collapse trajectory invisible to PR: $\\text{ISR}^{*}{:}\\,0.642\\to 0.132\\to 0.003$ over 100--1000 steps (99.5\\% degradation) while PR rises $32\\%{\\to}96\\%$.\nThree seeds diverge radically (PR $\\sigma{=}47$\\,pp), each converging to a qualitatively different mode: conclusion embedding, repetition collapse, and tautological collapse.\nAn SFT baseline on identical data reaches $\\text{PR}{=}13.0\\%$ with 92.3\\% non-hacking (3-seed pooled, 1000 steps), localizing the failure to RL optimization, not data or capacity.\n\n\\paragraph{Meta-level Goodhart.}\nRepurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR is gamed at its own definition---\\emph{meta-level Goodhart's Law}~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nAt $\\beta{=}0.1$, 70.6\\% of proved samples exhibit conclusion duplication (H7)---versus 13.7\\% under matched random (Fisher $p{<}10^{-38}$; random H7 is highly seed-dependent), showing ISR amplifies tautological collapse by ${\\sim}5{\\times}$; the one seed with a non-trivial residual reduces to $1.06\\times$ random after controlling for a premise-count confound.\nAt $\\beta{=}0.3$, both ISR and random produce extreme cross-seed instability (PR $66{\\pm}42\\%$ and $54{\\pm}35\\%$, respectively), confirming that high-dose proxy weighting is harmful regardless of signal content; ISR seeds diverge into three distinct pathways (reward collapse, shortcut dominance, pure metric gaming) while random converges uniformly to H1.\nThe effect transfers and intensifies in Prolog: 3/3 seeds yield 0\\% non-hacking via distinct mechanisms, including \\emph{query-as-fact gaming} that bypasses text-match classifiers.\nISR's asymmetry is the central finding: \\emph{reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.}\n\n\\paragraph{Contributions.}\nAcross 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 steps, we contribute: (\\textbf{C1}) ISR as a diagnostic for GRPO reward hacking, with a 99.5\\% collapse trajectory invisible to PR and an SFT control localizing the failure to RL (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); (\\textbf{C2}) empirical meta-level Goodhart, with a random control showing ISR amplifies H7 gaming ${\\sim}5{\\times}$ (70.6\\% vs.\\ 13.7\\%, Fisher $p{<}10^{-38}$; random H7 is seed-dependent) and a premise-count-matched analysis showing the sole apparent gain is an LOO artifact (\\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}); (\\textbf{C3}) cross-logic transfer to Prolog with universal Goodhart (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}); (\\textbf{C4}) an eight-mode hacking taxonomy including query-as-fact gaming and obfuscated tautology, motivating functional cross-validation of diagnostics (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy}).\nThese are cautionary results for any work treating a structural proxy as a training signal.\n",

    "newString": "(\\textbf{C2}) the first empirical evidence of Goodhart's Law at the meta level---a diagnostic gamed when optimized---with a random control showing",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nA GRPO-trained formalizer achieves a 93\\% prove rate on first-order logic problems---yet 87\\% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion verbatim among the premises.\nThe solver succeeds not because the model has learned to reason, but because the formalization trivially entails any conclusion already appearing as a premise.\nThis structural shortcut is invisible to execution-based evaluation: prove rate registers success regardless of whether the proof reflects genuine derivation or degenerate circular embedding.\n\n\\paragraph{The execution-only blind spot.}\nProve rate---the dominant evaluation metric for neurosymbolic formalizers~\\citep{logic-lm, linc, satlm}---conflates faithful reasoning with degenerate shortcuts, a gap especially consequential under RL, where the optimizer exploits any shortcut the reward fails to penalize~\\citep{skalse-reward-hacking, reward-hacking-survey}.\nRecent work applies GRPO~\\citep{deepseekmath} to train LLMs that generate solver-verified formal programs~\\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl} using binary execution feedback; this drives rapid PR gains but provides no signal on structural quality.\nProcess reward models~\\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} require expensive annotations and do not transfer to arbitrary solver-backed settings.\nA separate line of work documents that LLMs already game logical formalization at inference time~\\citep{kim-game-formalization, bondarenko-spec-gaming}; what is missing is an annotation-free \\emph{diagnostic} for structural integrity in neurosymbolic outputs.\n\n\\paragraph{Component necessity testing.}\nWe address this gap with \\emph{Solver Trace Ablation} (STA), a diagnostic applying \\emph{component necessity testing}---leave-one-out (LOO) ablation on solver inputs---to quantify structural integrity (Figure~\\ref{fig:sta-overview}).\nFor each proved formalization, STA removes each component individually and re-runs the solver; the fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure yields the \\emph{Intensional Structural Reward} (ISR), with $\\text{ISR}{=}1$ indicating full necessity and $\\text{ISR}{=}0$ complete redundancy.\nLOO ablation itself is standard~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; novelty lies in its application as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and in the empirical phenomena it surfaces.\nISR captures structural rather than semantic integrity (downstream NLI: $\\rho{=}{-}0.049$, $p{=}0.187$).\n\n\\paragraph{ISR as diagnostic.}\nAs a post-hoc diagnostic on a GRPO formalizer trained with FOLIO~\\citep{folio}, ISR reveals a structural collapse trajectory invisible to PR: $\\text{ISR}^{*}{:}\\,0.642\\to 0.132\\to 0.003$ over 100--1000 steps (99.5\\% degradation) while PR rises $32\\%{\\to}96\\%$.\nThree seeds diverge radically (PR $\\sigma{=}47$\\,pp), each converging to a qualitatively different mode: conclusion embedding, repetition collapse, and tautological collapse.\nAn SFT baseline on identical data reaches $\\text{PR}{=}13.0\\%$ with 92.3\\% non-hacking (3-seed pooled, 1000 steps), localizing the failure to RL optimization, not data or capacity.\n\n\\paragraph{Meta-level Goodhart.}\nRepurposed as a training signal, $r = r_{\\text{exec}} \\cdot (1 + \\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$, ISR is gamed at its own definition---an empirical instance of Goodhart's Law~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy} at the meta level, where the diagnostic designed to detect gaming becomes gamed when optimized~\\citep{karwowski-goodhart-rl, gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nAt $\\beta{=}0.1$, 70.6\\% of proved samples exhibit conclusion duplication (H7)---versus 13.7\\% under matched random (Fisher $p{<}10^{-38}$; random H7 is highly seed-dependent), showing ISR amplifies tautological collapse by ${\\sim}5{\\times}$; the one seed with a non-trivial residual reduces to $1.06\\times$ random after controlling for a premise-count confound.\nAt $\\beta{=}0.3$, both ISR and random produce extreme cross-seed instability (PR $66{\\pm}42\\%$ and $54{\\pm}35\\%$, respectively), confirming that high-dose proxy weighting is harmful regardless of signal content; ISR seeds diverge into three distinct pathways (reward collapse, shortcut dominance, pure metric gaming) while random converges uniformly to H1.\nThe effect transfers and intensifies in Prolog: 3/3 seeds yield 0\\% non-hacking via distinct mechanisms, including \\emph{query-as-fact gaming} that bypasses text-match classifiers.\nISR's asymmetry is the central finding: \\emph{reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.}\n\n\\paragraph{Contributions.}\nAcross 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 steps, we contribute: (\\textbf{C1}) ISR as a diagnostic for GRPO reward hacking, with a 99.5\\% collapse trajectory invisible to PR and an SFT control localizing the failure to RL (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); (\\textbf{C2}) empirical meta-level Goodhart, with a random control showing ISR amplifies H7 gaming ${\\sim}5{\\times}$ (70.6\\% vs.\\ 13.7\\%, Fisher $p{<}10^{-38}$; random H7 is seed-dependent) and a premise-count-matched analysis showing the sole apparent gain is an LOO artifact (\\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}); (\\textbf{C3}) cross-logic transfer to Prolog with universal Goodhart (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}); (\\textbf{C4}) an eight-mode hacking taxonomy including query-as-fact gaming and obfuscated tautology, motivating functional cross-validation of diagnostics (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy}).\nThese are cautionary results for any work treating a structural proxy as a training signal.\n",
