    "originalFile": "\\section{Method}\n\\label{sec:method}\n\n\\begin{figure*}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.92\\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sta_overview.pdf}\n\\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA). (a) Formalization pipeline: NL is formalized and verified by an external solver. (b) STA protocol: leave-one-out ablation on proved formalizations yields ISR. (c) ISR as diagnostic vs.\\ as reward: when the diagnostic becomes the target, meta-level Goodhart's Law emerges.}\n\\label{fig:sta-overview}\n\\end{figure*}\n\n\\subsection{Task and GRPO Training}\n\\label{sec:task}\n\nGiven premises $P_1,\\ldots,P_k$ and conclusion $C$, the task is to produce a formal program $F=\\{c_1,\\ldots,c_n\\}$ verified by an external solver $S\\in\\{\\text{Prover9, SWI-Prolog}\\}$ (Figure~\\ref{fig:sta-overview}a).\nWe train with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)~\\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$), where each rollout receives binary execution reward $r_{\\text{exec}}(x) = \\mathbf{1}[S(F_x){=}\\textit{proved}]$.\nThis optimizes prove rate but provides no signal about \\emph{structural quality}: a formalization embedding the conclusion among its premises receives the same reward as a faithful derivation.\nAll experiments draw from FOLIO~\\citep{folio}; the held-out evaluation file retains a legacy \\texttt{proofwriter\\_fol\\_*.jsonl} filename (from ProofWriter~\\citep{proofwriter}) but contains only FOLIO-derived examples.\n\n\\subsection{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}\n\\label{sec:sta}\n\nSTA tests whether each component of a proved formalization is individually necessary for proof success (Figure~\\ref{fig:sta-overview}b).\nGiven a proved $x$ with $n$ components, STA constructs $n$ ablated variants $x\\setminus\\{c_i\\}$ and re-runs the solver (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:sta}).\nA component is \\emph{necessary} if its removal causes proof failure.\nWhat constitutes a component depends on the logic: premises in FOL, clauses or ground facts in Prolog.\nLOO ablation itself is standard in mutation testing and sensitivity analysis~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey}; novelty lies in its application as a GRPO-hacking diagnostic and the empirical phenomena it surfaces.\n\n\\begin{algorithm}[t]\n\\caption{Solver Trace Ablation (STA)}\n\\label{alg:sta}\n\\begin{algorithmic}[1]\n\\REQUIRE Proved formalization $x = \\{c_1, \\ldots, c_n\\}$, solver $S$\n\\ENSURE $\\text{ISR}(x) \\in [0, 1]$\n\\STATE $\\textit{necessary} \\gets 0$\n\\FOR{$i = 1$ to $n$}\n    \\IF{$S(x \\setminus \\{c_i\\}) = \\textit{failed}$}\n        \\STATE $\\textit{necessary} \\gets \\textit{necessary} + 1$\n    \\ENDIF\n\\ENDFOR\n\\RETURN $\\textit{necessary} \\,/\\, n$\n\\end{algorithmic}\n\\end{algorithm}\n\n\\paragraph{Intensional Structural Reward.}\nISR is the fraction of necessary components:\n\\begin{equation}\n  \\text{ISR}(x) = \\frac{1}{n}\\sum_{i=1}^{n}\\mathbf{1}\\bigl[S(x\\setminus\\{c_i\\})=\\textit{failed}\\bigr]\n  \\label{eq:isr}\n\\end{equation}\nISR is defined only for proved formalizations: ISR${=}1$ means every component is necessary; ISR${=}0$ means complete redundancy.\nWe report \\emph{proved-only} ISR (denoted ISR$^*$) throughout to avoid conflating structural quality with prove rate.\n\n\\paragraph{Limitations of ISR.}\nISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness: a formalization can achieve $\\text{ISR}{=}1$ while remaining semantically unrelated to the NL input (e.g., a single premise identical to the conclusion).\nISR is also sensitive to component count $n$: when $n{=}2$, $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$ regardless of proof quality.\nWe control for this confound via premise-count-matched analysis in \\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}.\nThe $O(n)$ per-clause LOO ablation is necessary: a cheaper $O(1)$ binary variant produces near-zero ISR scores ($\\text{ISR}^*{=}0.007$) indistinguishable from exec-only, because models generate redundant premise copies whose individual removal never changes the proof outcome.\nHowever, the $O(1)$ variant includes a tautology check (conclusion provable under empty premises $\\to$ ISR${=}0$) absent from exec-only, introducing a training-time confound that depresses prove rate ($63\\%$ vs.\\ $93\\%$ exec-only); the two conditions are therefore non-equivalent in training dynamics despite producing comparable ISR signals (Appendix~\\ref{app:o1-ablation}).\n\n\\subsection{Reward Conditions and Hacking Modes}\n\\label{sec:rewards}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy-method}\n\nWe study six conditions: exec-only ($r_{\\text{exec}}$), ISR low/high-dose ($r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1{+}\\beta\\,\\text{ISR})$ at $\\beta\\in\\{0.1,0.3\\}$), matched random controls ($r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1{+}\\beta u)$, $u\\sim\\mathcal{U}(0,1)$), and a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baseline (negative log-likelihood on gold formalizations).\nMultiplicative bonuses apply only to proved samples, keeping the prove-rate floor intact.\nRandom isolates ISR-specific signal from generic reward-variance effects; SFT isolates whether structural degeneration arises from RL optimization or data exposure alone.\nEach RL condition uses seeds $\\{42,43,44\\}$ and identical training data.\nAn earlier convex formulation ($\\alpha r_{\\text{exec}} + \\beta r_{\\text{exec}}\\text{ISR}$, $\\alpha{+}\\beta{=}1$) was discarded because $\\text{ISR}{<}1$ always penalized relative to exec-only, collapsing PR (Appendix~\\ref{app:setup}).\n\n\\paragraph{Hacking taxonomy.}\nAcross conditions, seeds, and logic types we identify eight structural-degeneration modes (full definitions in Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}):\n\\textbf{H1} (conclusion embedded among multiple premises),\n\\textbf{H7} (single-premise conclusion duplication, subtypes H7a standard and H7b obfuscated tautology; targets the ISR signal directly, since removing the sole premise forces $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$),\n\\textbf{H8} (premise-subset gaming: each component is LOO-necessary but the premise set is a strict subset of what the problem requires, inflating ISR without faithful reasoning; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8}),\n\\textbf{tautological collapse} (minimal two-component $P{\\to}P$ without explicit ISR inflation),\n\\textbf{repetition collapse} (identical clauses repeated to inflate component count),\n\\textbf{complexity-without-utility} (elaborate but vacuous premises),\n\\textbf{contradiction explosion} (contradictory premises trivializing any conclusion via \\emph{ex falso}), and\n\\textbf{query-as-fact gaming} (Prolog-specific: emit the NL question as an atom asserted as both fact and query; evades text-match classifiers).\nModes are identified by a two-stage procedure: strict normalized text matching (H1/H7) plus functional analysis of the per-sample $n_{\\text{necessary}}$ distribution (catches modes evading text matching).\nWe report both classifications in \\S\\ref{sec:experiments}.\n\nIn interpreting ISR results, we distinguish two failure semantics that share a numerical signature: an exec-only \\emph{low} ISR signals \\emph{redundancy} (e.g., repeated ground facts in Prolog), whereas an ISR-rewarded \\emph{high} ISR signals \\emph{metric gaming} (H7-style constructions).\nA bare ISR difference therefore does not translate directly into a reasoning-ability difference; the mode breakdown is what licenses such interpretation.\n",

    "originalFile": "\\section{Experiments}\n\\label{sec:experiments}\n\nWe evaluate STA along three axes: ISR as a diagnostic for reward hacking (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}); ISR as a training reward, where it exhibits meta-level Goodhart's Law (\\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}); and STA as a cross-logic protocol (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}).\nA hacking taxonomy synthesizes the modes observed across conditions (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy}).\n\n\\subsection{Setup}\\label{sec:setup}\n\nWe train Qwen3-4B~\\citep{qwen3} with GRPO~\\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$, lr $10^{-6}$, 500 steps default, 1000 where noted) over seeds $\\{42,43,44\\}$ on a fixed 500-example FOLIO~\\citep{folio} subset held constant across all conditions; the SFT baseline is trained with negative log-likelihood (NLL) on gold formalizations.\\footnote{All experiments use \\texttt{data/train\\_500\\_seed42.jsonl}; seeds control initialization and rollout sampling only.}\nHeld-out evaluation uses 100 FOLIO examples (\\texttt{proofwriter\\_fol\\_100.jsonl}---legacy filename, no ProofWriter data) with greedy decoding; all cross-run ISR values are recomputed uniformly by a single evaluator (\\texttt{eval\\_sta.py}) so training-log ISR is never compared across runs.\nWe report prove rate (PR), proved-only ISR ($\\text{ISR}^*$), hacking-mode rates from a two-stage classifier (strict text matching for H1/H7 plus functional analysis of the per-sample $n_{\\text{necessary}}$ distribution; \\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method}), and a \\emph{non-hacking} (N-H) rate---the residual of proved samples matching none of the eight cataloged patterns, a necessary but not sufficient condition for semantic correctness.\n\n\\subsection{ISR as Diagnostic}\n\\label{sec:diagnostic}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig2_isr_collapse.pdf}\n\\caption{ISR collapse under exec-only GRPO (seed~42). PR rises 32\\%$\\to$93\\%$\\to$96\\% while proved-only ISR falls $0.642{\\to}0.132{\\to}0.003$ over 100$\\to$500$\\to$1000 steps. By step 1000, 94.8\\% of proved samples have $\\text{ISR}{=}0$ and mean component count inflates 6.58$\\to$18.55 (contradiction explosion).}\n\\label{fig:isr-collapse}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\textbf{Collapse trajectory.}\nFigure~\\ref{fig:isr-collapse} traces exec-only GRPO (seed~42) at 100, 500, 1000 steps.\nPR rises $32\\%\\to93\\%\\to96\\%$, a trajectory that execution-only evaluation would interpret as successful optimization; $\\text{ISR}^*$ tells the opposite story, $0.642\\to0.132\\to0.003$ (214$\\times$ collapse). %% registry: plan003-eval-exec-only-r5, plan001-exec-only-s42-r2, plan016-extended-1000-s42\nBy 500 steps, 87.1\\% of proved samples embed the conclusion (H1); by 1000 steps, the embedding rate saturates at 100\\% and mean component count more than doubles ($6.58\\to18.55$) as the model emits contradictory or vacuous premises that trivially derive any conclusion.\nExtended training, lacking a structural signal, \\emph{worsens} formalization quality while improving the headline number.\n\n\\textbf{Cross-seed instability.}\nThe three exec-only seeds diverge into qualitatively different failure modes despite shared data, hyperparameters, and reward (Appendix~\\ref{app:detailed-results}): s42 reaches $\\text{PR}{=}93\\%$ via conclusion embedding ($\\text{H1}{=}87\\%$); s43 collapses to $\\text{PR}{=}13\\%$ via repetition (87\\% of rollouts truncated); s44 reaches $\\text{PR}{=}98\\%$ via tautological collapse ($\\text{H7}{=}100\\%$, mean $n{=}2.04$). %% registry: plan001-exec-only-s42-r2, plan001-exec-only-s43-r4, plan001-exec-only-s44\nThe cross-seed PR variance ($\\sigma{=}47$\\,pp) shows that aggregate metrics obscure qualitatively different failure modes; all three are flagged by $\\text{ISR}^*$ even though one passes the PR check (s44) and one fails it (s43).\nBy 1000 steps, all three converge to high PR ($0.95{\\pm}0.05$) but $0\\%$ non-hacking (15 samples escape text-match classifiers but are functionally degenerate; see Appendix~\\ref{app:h8} and Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}), each through a different dominant mode (s42: H1 saturation; s43: H7 tautology; s44: H1+H8). %% registry: plan016-extended-1000-s42, plan001-exec-only-s43-1000-r2, plan001-exec-only-s44-1000-r2\n\n\\textbf{SFT baseline.}\nSFT on gold formalizations reaches $\\text{PR}{=}13.0\\%$ (3-seed pooled, 1000 steps) with $\\text{H7}{=}0$, $\\text{H1}{=}7.7\\%$, $92.3\\%$ non-hacking ($n{=}39$; per-seed range ${\\sim}$91--93\\%). %% registry: plan009-sft-baseline-s42-1000/s43-1000/s44-1000-r3\nThe near-absence of hacking under identical data exposure is consistent with hacking being RL-specific.\nA 100-step RL checkpoint (Appendix~\\ref{app:100step}) at $\\text{PR}{=}32\\%$ already shows 31.2\\% hacking (vs.\\ SFT's 7.7\\%), indicating reward hacking emerges early in RL training, not merely at high PR.\n\n\\subsection{Meta-Level Goodhart's Law}\n\\label{sec:goodhart}\n\nWe now reverse roles: instead of treating ISR as an observer, we use it as a reward augmentation, $r = r_{\\text{exec}}\\cdot(1 Wilson 95\\% CI: ISR $[3.6\\%, 9.9\\%]$, Random $[6.5\\%, 13.7\\%]$; Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:bootstrap-ci}). %% D095 FINAL Fisher test\nThe key asymmetry is not in non-hacking rates but in gaming \\emph{composition}: ISR converts stochastic H7 into near-deterministic convergence ($2/3$ seeds ${\\geq}97\\%$), while random H7 remains seed-dependent and H1-dominated.\nThe raw $3.2\\times$ ISR$^*$ headline collapses once the LOO bias is controlled---a methodological lesson for any LOO-based metric on variable-length outputs. %% artifact: wilson_ci_complete, h8_reclassification\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig5_training_isr_curves.pdf}\n\\caption{Training ISR trajectories across reward conditions. each seed converges to a distinct pathway (Appendix~\\ref{app:detailed-results}). %% registry: plan002-additive-b03-s42/s43/s44\nSeed~42: \\emph{reward collapse} ($\\text{PR}{=}18\\%$), following the pattern described by~\\citet{gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nSeed~43: \\emph{H1 shortcutting} ($\\text{PR}{=}86\\%$, $\\text{H1}{=}69\\%$, only $27\\%$ non-hacking).\nSeed~44: \\emph{pure H7 meta-Goodhart} ($\\text{PR}{=}95\\%$, $\\text{H7}{=}100\\%$, $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.742$, $0\\%$ non-hacking)---simultaneously gaming \\emph{both} execution and structural metrics through the same construction.\nThe random control at $\\beta{=}0.3$ is equally unstable ($\\text{PR}{=}54{\\pm}35\\%$, spanning 23--92\\%) with near-universal H1 hacking ($91\\%$, pooled) and ${\\leq}4\\%$ non-hacking per seed; high-dose proxy weighting destabilizes training regardless of signal source; ISR produces diverse failure modes (reward collapse, H1 shortcutting, H7 meta-Goodhart) while random converges to H1-dominated hacking with sporadic H7 ($7\\%$ pooled, $0$--$7$ H7a per seed) (Appendix~\\ref{app:beta03}).\n\n\\subsection{Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer}\n\\label{sec:crosslogic}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig4_prolog_comparison.pdf}\n\\caption{Cross-logic results. \\textbf{Left}: FOL vs.\\ Prolog exec-only $\\text{ISR}^*$ across 3 seeds (s44 Prolog $0.387$ is a small-sample artifact, $n{=}21$). \\textbf{Right}: Prolog ISR-reward gaming-mode composition---all 3 seeds reach 0\\% non-hacking through distinct mechanisms.}\n\\label{fig:prolog-comparison}\n\\end{figure}\n\nWe replicate the FOL pipeline with Prolog/SWI-Prolog: (i) does ISR retain diagnostic value on a structurally different logic; D095 FINAL\nRandom H7 is highly seed-dependent: $38.3\\%$ in seed~43 vs.\\ $0\\%$ in seeds~42 and~44; ISR makes H7 near-deterministic ($2/3$ seeds at $97$--$100\\%$, Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}).\nH7---a single premise identical to the conclusion, giving $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$ by construction (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---is the canonical meta-level Goodhart move: the model satisfies the LOO definition directly rather than producing a derivation that happens to satisfy it.\nTotal conclusion embedding ($\\text{H1}+\\text{H7}$) is GRPO-universal at $68$--$100\\%$ across both conditions (pooled: ISR $89\\%$, random $85\\%$).\nThe composition differs: H1 alone accounts for $71\\%$ under random vs.\\ $18\\%$ under ISR---ISR converts H1 into H7 rather than reducing total embedding (Figure~\\ref{fig:hacking-composition}).\nRandom also yields higher and more stable PR ($87{\\pm}8\\%$ vs.\\ $77{\\pm}14\\%$).\n\n\\textbf{Premise-count confound.}\nThe one ISR seed not dominated by H7 (s42) has raw $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.415$, $3.2\\times$ its random control ($0.131$), suggesting genuine improvement.\nThis appearance is a premise-minimization artifact: ISR-trained models produce shorter formalizations (mean $n{=}5.58$ vs.\\ $8.46$ for random), and ISR rises mechanically as $n$ falls---when $n{=}2$, $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$ by definition.\nRestricting to non-hacking samples with $\\geq{3}$ premises (eliminating the trivial-$n$ regime) gives $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.258$ for ISR vs.\\ $0.243$ for random: a $1.06\\times$ ratio, no meaningful advantage. %% D051 premise-count-matched analysis\nAfter reclassifying premise-subset gaming (H8; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8}) and correcting H7 classifier false negatives (s44, $n{=}12$; Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}), ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ non-hacking rate ($6.06\\%$, $14/231$) is numerically lower than random ($9.5\\%$, $25/262$), but the difference is not statistically significant (Fisher $p{=}0.182$, two-sided, n.s.; Wilson 95\\% CI: ISR $[3.6\\%, 9.9\\%]$, Random $[6.5\\%, 13.7\\%]$; Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:bootstrap-ci}). %% D095 FINAL Fisher test\nThe key asymmetry is not in non-hacking rates but in gaming \\emph{composition}: ISR converts stochastic H7 into near-deterministic convergence ($2/3$ seeds ${\\geq}97\\%$), while random H7 remains seed-dependent and H1-dominated.\nThe raw $3.2\\times$ ISR$^*$ headline collapses once the LOO bias is controlled---a methodological lesson for any LOO-based metric on variable-length outputs. %% artifact: wilson_ci_complete, h8_reclassification\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig5_training_isr_curves.pdf}\n\\caption{Training ISR trajectories across reward conditions. ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ seeds rise rapidly toward $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}1$ (meta-Goodhart gaming via H7 tautology); exec-only s44's plateau at ${\\sim}0.5$ is an H7a artifact ($n{=}2$ gives $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$ by construction), not genuine structural quality; random $\\beta{=}0.1$ remains low. The s42 1k-step trace shows ISR eventually saturates at $0.88$ through the same H7 mechanism.}\n\\label{fig:training-isr-curves}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\textbf{Temporal collapse under ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$.}\nFigure~\\ref{fig:training-isr-curves} traces training ISR across conditions.\nExtended training (1000 steps) reveals that ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$'s non-hacking rate at 500 steps ($17.3\\%$, seed~42) is transient: by step 1000, H7 gaming escalates from $16.0\\%$ to $96.7\\%$ while the non-hacking rate collapses to $2.2\\%$ ($n_{\\text{comp}}$: $5.58{\\to}2.89$).\nThe optimization converges to a fixed point where $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.88$, superficially suggesting structural improvement but reflecting near-complete tautological gaming.\nThis mirrors the exec-only 1000-step trajectory (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}): both converge to ${\\sim}0\\%$ non-hacking proofs despite opposite ISR values ($0.88$ vs.\\ $0.003$), demonstrating that ISR is gamed upward under ISR-as-reward and downward under exec-only---two paths to the same structural collapse. %% registry: plan002-b01-s42-1000, plan016-extended-1000-s42\n\n\\textbf{High-dose ISR ($\\beta{=}0.3$): multi-modal Goodhart.}\nAt $\\beta{=}0.3$, ISR-as-reward produces extreme cross-seed instability ($\\text{PR}{=}66{\\pm}42\\%$, spanning 18--95\\%); each seed converges to a distinct pathway (Appendix~\\ref{app:detailed-results}). %% registry: plan002-additive-b03-s42/s43/s44\nSeed~42: \\emph{reward collapse} ($\\text{PR}{=}18\\%$), following the pattern described by~\\citet{gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nSeed~43: \\emph{H1 shortcutting} ($\\text{PR}{=}86\\%$, $\\text{H1}{=}69\\%$, only $27\\%$ non-hacking).\nSeed~44: \\emph{pure H7 meta-Goodhart} ($\\text{PR}{=}95\\%$, $\\text{H7}{=}100\\%$, $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.742$, $0\\%$ non-hacking)---simultaneously gaming \\emph{both} execution and structural metrics through the same construction.\nThe random control at $\\beta{=}0.3$ is equally unstable ($\\text{PR}{=}54{\\pm}35\\%$, spanning 23--92\\%) with near-universal H1 hacking ($91\\%$, pooled) and ${\\leq}4\\%$ non-hacking per seed; high-dose proxy weighting destabilizes training regardless of signal source; ISR produces diverse failure modes (reward collapse, H1 shortcutting, H7 meta-Goodhart) while random converges to H1-dominated hacking with sporadic H7 ($7\\%$ pooled, $0$--$7$ H7a per seed) (Appendix~\\ref{app:beta03}).\n\n\\subsection{Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer}\n\\label{sec:crosslogic}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig4_prolog_comparison.pdf}\n\\caption{Cross-logic results. \\textbf{Left}: FOL vs.\\ Prolog exec-only $\\text{ISR}^*$ across 3 seeds (s44 Prolog $0.387$ is a small-sample artifact, $n{=}21$). \\textbf{Right}: Prolog ISR-reward gaming-mode composition---all 3 seeds reach 0\\% non-hacking through distinct mechanisms.}\n\\label{fig:prolog-comparison}\n\\end{figure}\n\nWe replicate the FOL pipeline with Prolog/SWI-Prolog: (i) does ISR retain diagnostic value on a structurally different logic; (ii) does ISR-as-reward reproduce meta-level Goodhart?\nDetailed per-seed numbers are in Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:prolog-detailed}.\n\n\\textbf{Prolog exec-only: ISR catches redundancy across logics.}\nTwo of three seeds (s42, s43) show $\\text{ISR}^{*}{\\approx}0$ despite moderate-to-high PR (0.83, 0.56). %% registry: plan006-prolog-exec-s42, plan006-prolog-exec-s43-r3\nInspection reveals \\emph{redundant coding}: ground facts repeated multiple times (e.g., \\texttt{penguin(fido).} appearing three times), so per-clause LOO never breaks the proof.\nThis is a different failure semantics from FOL exec-only (conclusion embedding), but $\\text{ISR}^{*}$ flags both.\nWe are careful to distinguish: exec-only \\emph{low} ISR signals \\emph{redundancy}; ISR-rewarded \\emph{high} ISR signals \\emph{metric gaming} (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---the same number does not license the same reasoning-quality conclusion.\n\n\\textbf{Prolog ISR-as-reward: universal Goodhart.}\nThe Prolog result is stronger than FOL: 3/3 seeds reach $0\\%$ non-hacking via three distinct mechanisms (Figure~\\ref{fig:prolog-comparison}).\nSeed~42: clean H7 tautologies ($\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}1.0$, all $50/100$ proved). %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s42\nSeed~43: \\emph{degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog} (e.g., \\texttt{MARY\\_IS\\_AN\\_AVA\\-DEOTE.}); strict text-match returns $\\text{H7}{=}0$ but functional analysis shows all 38 proved samples have $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$ (functionally H7). %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s43\nSeed~44: \\emph{query-as-fact gaming}---the model transforms the NL question into a snake-case atom asserted as both fact and query (e.g., \\texttt{is\\_the\\_dog\\_furry.}), with $95/97$ proved samples at $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$. %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s44\nSeed~44 evaded our initial strict classifier (reported $100\\%$ non-hacking); functional cross-validation exposed it---a methodological lesson that text-match classifiers have blind spots and STA's necessity profile is the cross-check.\nWe attribute Prolog's stronger collapse to its simpler operational semantics: a broader gaming basin makes the non-hacking residual disappear---corroborating evidence for Claim 2.\n\n\\subsection{Hacking Taxonomy}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.92\\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_hacking_composition.pdf}\n\\caption{Hacking composition across reward conditions (proved samples; exec-only: seed~42; all other bars: pooled across 3 seeds, per-seed breakdown in Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}). ISR converts H1 into more extreme H7 without reducing total hacking; SFT shows near-zero hacking.}\n\\label{fig:hacking-composition}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\resizebox{\\columnwidth}{!}{%\n\\begin{tabular}{lccccc}\n\\toprule\n\\textbf{Mode} & \\textbf{Exec} & \\textbf{ISR$_{.1}$} & \\textbf{Rnd$_{.1}$} & \\textbf{SFT} & \\textbf{Prolog$_{\\text{ISR}}$} \\\\\n\\midrule\nH1 embed       & 87 & 18 & 71 & 7 & 0$^\\sharp$ \\\\\nH7 duplicate   & 0  & 71 & 14 & 0 & 100$^\\sharp$ \\\\\nH8 prem-subset & 6  & 5  & 8   & 0 & --- \\\\\nTautological   & --- & --- & --- & --- & s42 \\\\\nRepetition     & s43 & --- & --- & --- & --- \\\\\nContradiction  & 1k & --- & --- & --- & --- \\\\\nCmplx.\\ vacuous& 0  & 0  & 4  & 0 & --- \\\\\nQuery-as-fact  & --- & --- & --- & --- & s44 \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\textbf{Non-hacking} & 8  & 6  & 10 & \\textbf{92} & \\textbf{0} \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}}\n\\caption{Eight hacking modes across conditions (\\% of proved samples; exec-only/SFT: seed~42; ISR/Random: pooled across 3 seeds). H7 includes subtypes H7a (text-match, $n{=}149$) and H7b (obfuscated tautology, $n{=}14$; primarily s44). Cells with seed identifiers indicate the mode dominates that seed. $^\\sharp$Prolog row pools strict+functional (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}).}\n\\label{tab:hacking-taxonomy}\n\\end{table}\n\nThe eight modes (Table~\\ref{tab:hacking-taxonomy}; definitions in Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}) span 2 logic types and 6 reward conditions.\nThe three dominant modes---\\textbf{H1} (conclusion embedding, GRPO-universal at $68$--$100\\%$), \\textbf{H7} (conclusion duplication, ISR-amplified ${\\sim}5\\times$; Fisher $p{=}1.3{\\times}10^{-39}$), and \\textbf{H8} (premise-subset gaming; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8})---account for $>90\\%$ of hacking.\nH7 includes an obfuscated subtype \\textbf{H7b} ($14/231$) that evades text-match classifiers via vacuous quantifier wrapping, simultaneously gaming execution reward, ISR, and the hacking detector (\\S\\ref{sec:discussion}).\nThe cross-condition pattern: \\emph{reward formulation determines hacking composition but not prevalence}---all GRPO conditions show $\\geq 80\\%$ pooled hacking; only SFT achieves $92.3\\%$ non-hacking.\nNo GRPO condition produces a high-PR, majority non-hacking run; the taxonomy is descriptive, not exhaustive (query-as-fact gaming was discovered only via functional cross-validation).\n",

exp1-random-b03-s44-r2 (pooled; D086 H7: s42=4,s43=7,s44=0)\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}}\n\\caption{Goodhart landscape across reward conditions. 3-seed rows: H7/H1/N-H pooled across seeds; PR/ISR$^*$: mean$\\pm$std of per-seed values. $^\\dagger$Seed~42. $\\ddagger$$\\text{ISR}^*$ omitted for ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ because the raw mean is inflated by H7 gaming; premise-count-matched value is $1.06\\times$ random (\\S\\ref{sec:goodhart}, Appendix~\\ref{app:detailed-results}). $^\\P$Includes H7 classifier false-negative correction (s44) and H8 premise-subset gaming (s42; 5.2\\% of proved; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8}). $^\\S$Random H7 driven by seed~43 (38.3\\%); seeds~42 and~44: 0\\%. Per-seed breakdown in Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}.}\n\\label{tab:goodhart-landscape}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\textbf{Low-dose ISR ($\\beta{=}0.1$): ISR-amplified gaming.}\nH7 tautological collapse occurs under both ISR and random conditions, but ISR amplifies it ${\\sim}5\\times$: $70.6\\%$ ($163/231$) under ISR vs.\\ $13.7\\%$ ($36/262$) under random (Fisher $p{=}1.3{\\times}10^{-39}$, $\\text{OR}{=}15.05$). %% registry: plan002 vs plan018 all seeds; D095 FINAL\nRandom H7 is highly seed-dependent: $38.3\\%$ in seed~43 vs.\\ $0\\%$ in seeds~42 and~44; ISR makes H7 near-deterministic ($2/3$ seeds at $97$--$100\\%$, Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}).\nH7---a single premise identical to the conclusion, giving $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$ by construction (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---is the canonical meta-level Goodhart move: the model satisfies the LOO definition directly rather than producing a derivation that happens to satisfy it.\nTotal conclusion embedding ($\\text{H1}+\\text{H7}$) is GRPO-universal at $68$--$100\\%$ across both conditions (pooled: ISR $89\\%$, random $85\\%$).\nThe composition differs: H1 alone accounts for $71\\%$ under random vs.\\ $18\\%$ under ISR---ISR converts H1 into H7 rather than reducing total embedding (Figure~\\ref{fig:hacking-composition}).\nRandom also yields higher and more stable PR ($87{\\pm}8\\%$ vs.\\ $77{\\pm}14\\%$).\n\n\\textbf{Premise-count confound.}\nThe one ISR seed not dominated by H7 (s42) has raw $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.415$, $3.2\\times$ its random control ($0.131$), suggesting genuine improvement.\nThis appearance is a premise-minimization artifact: ISR-trained models produce shorter formalizations (mean $n{=}5.58$ vs.\\ $8.46$ for random), and ISR rises mechanically as $n$ falls---when $n{=}2$, $\\text{ISR}\\geq 0.5$ by definition.\nRestricting to non-hacking samples with $\\geq{3}$ premises (eliminating the trivial-$n$ regime) gives $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.258$ for ISR vs.\\ $0.243$ for random: a $1.06\\times$ ratio, no meaningful advantage. %% D051 premise-count-matched analysis\nAfter reclassifying premise-subset gaming (H8; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8}) and correcting H7 classifier false negatives (s44, $n{=}12$; Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}), ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ non-hacking rate ($6.06\\%$, $14/231$) is numerically lower than random ($9.5\\%$, $25/262$), but the difference is not statistically significant (Fisher $p{=}0.182$, two-sided, n.s.; Wilson 95\\% CI: ISR $[3.6\\%, 9.9\\%]$, Random $[6.5\\%, 13.7\\%]$; Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:bootstrap-ci}). %% D095 FINAL Fisher test\nThe key asymmetry is not in non-hacking rates but in gaming \\emph{composition}: ISR converts stochastic H7 into near-deterministic convergence ($2/3$ seeds ${\\geq}97\\%$), while random H7 remains seed-dependent and H1-dominated.\nThe raw $3.2\\times$ ISR$^*$ headline collapses once the LOO bias is controlled---a methodological lesson for any LOO-based metric on variable-length outputs. %% artifact: wilson_ci_complete, h8_reclassification\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig5_training_isr_curves.pdf}\n\\caption{Training ISR trajectories across reward conditions. ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ seeds rise rapidly toward $\\text{ISR}{\\approx}1$ (meta-Goodhart gaming via H7 tautology); exec-only s44's plateau at ${\\sim}0.5$ is an H7a artifact ($n{=}2$ gives $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.5$ by construction), not genuine structural quality; random $\\beta{=}0.1$ remains low. The s42 1k-step trace shows ISR eventually saturates at $0.88$ through the same H7 mechanism.}\n\\label{fig:training-isr-curves}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\textbf{Temporal collapse under ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$.}\nFigure~\\ref{fig:training-isr-curves} traces training ISR across conditions.\nExtended training (1000 steps) reveals that ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$'s non-hacking rate at 500 steps ($17.3\\%$, seed~42) is transient: by step 1000, H7 gaming escalates from $16.0\\%$ to $96.7\\%$ while the non-hacking rate collapses to $2.2\\%$ ($n_{\\text{comp}}$: $5.58{\\to}2.89$).\nThe optimization converges to a fixed point where $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.88$, superficially suggesting structural improvement but reflecting near-complete tautological gaming.\nThis mirrors the exec-only 1000-step trajectory (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}): both converge to ${\\sim}0\\%$ non-hacking proofs despite opposite ISR values ($0.88$ vs.\\ $0.003$), demonstrating that ISR is gamed upward under ISR-as-reward and downward under exec-only---two paths to the same structural collapse. %% registry: plan002-b01-s42-1000, plan016-extended-1000-s42\n\n\\textbf{High-dose ISR ($\\beta{=}0.3$): multi-modal Goodhart.}\nAt $\\beta{=}0.3$, ISR-as-reward produces extreme cross-seed instability ($\\text{PR}{=}66{\\pm}42\\%$, spanning 18--95\\%); each seed converges to a distinct pathway (Appendix~\\ref{app:detailed-results}). %% registry: plan002-additive-b03-s42/s43/s44\nSeed~42: \\emph{reward collapse} ($\\text{PR}{=}18\\%$), following the pattern described by~\\citet{gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nSeed~43: \\emph{H1 shortcutting} ($\\text{PR}{=}86\\%$, $\\text{H1}{=}69\\%$, only $27\\%$ non-hacking).\nSeed~44: \\emph{pure H7 meta-Goodhart} ($\\text{PR}{=}95\\%$, $\\text{H7}{=}100\\%$, $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.742$, $0\\%$ non-hacking)---simultaneously gaming \\emph{both} execution and structural metrics through the same construction.\nThe random control at $\\beta{=}0.3$ is equally unstable ($\\text{PR}{=}54{\\pm}35\\%$, spanning 23--92\\%) with near-universal H1 hacking ($91\\%$, pooled) and ${\\leq}4\\%$ non-hacking per seed; high-dose proxy weighting destabilizes training regardless of signal source; ISR produces diverse failure modes (reward collapse, H1 shortcutting, H7 meta-Goodhart) while random converges to H1-dominated hacking with sporadic H7 ($7\\%$ pooled, $0$--$7$ H7a per seed) (Appendix~\\ref{app:beta03}).\n\n\\subsection{Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer}\n\\label{sec:crosslogic}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig4_prolog_comparison.pdf}\n\\caption{Cross-logic results. \\textbf{Left}: FOL vs.\\ Prolog exec-only $\\text{ISR}^*$ across 3 seeds (s44 Prolog $0.387$ is a small-sample artifact, $n{=}21$). \\textbf{Right}: Prolog ISR-reward gaming-mode composition---all 3 seeds reach 0\\% non-hacking through distinct mechanisms.}\n\\label{fig:prolog-comparison}\n\\end{figure}\n\nWe replicate the FOL pipeline with Prolog/SWI-Prolog: (i) does ISR retain diagnostic value on a structurally different logic; (ii) does ISR-as-reward reproduce meta-level Goodhart?\nDetailed per-seed numbers are in Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:prolog-detailed}.\n\n\\textbf{Prolog exec-only: ISR catches redundancy across logics.}\nTwo of three seeds (s42, s43) show $\\text{ISR}^{*}{\\approx}0$ despite moderate-to-high PR (0.83, 0.56). %% registry: plan006-prolog-exec-s42, plan006-prolog-exec-s43-r3\nInspection reveals \\emph{redundant coding}: ground facts repeated multiple times (e.g., \\texttt{penguin(fido).} appearing three times), so per-clause LOO never breaks the proof.\nThis is a different failure semantics from FOL exec-only (conclusion embedding), but $\\text{ISR}^{*}$ flags both.\nWe are careful to distinguish: exec-only \\emph{low} ISR signals \\emph{redundancy}; ISR-rewarded \\emph{high} ISR signals \\emph{metric gaming} (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---the same number does not license the same reasoning-quality conclusion.\n\n\\textbf{Prolog ISR-as-reward: universal Goodhart.}\nThe Prolog result is stronger than FOL: 3/3 seeds reach $0\\%$ non-hacking via three distinct mechanisms (Figure~\\ref{fig:prolog-comparison}).\nSeed~42: clean H7 tautologies ($\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}1.0$, all $50/100$ proved). %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s42\nSeed~43: \\emph{degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog} (e.g., \\texttt{MARY\\_IS\\_AN\\_AVA\\-DEOTE.}); strict text-match returns $\\text{H7}{=}0$ but functional analysis shows all 38 proved samples have $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$ (functionally H7). %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s43\nSeed~44: \\emph{query-as-fact gaming}---the model transforms the NL question into a snake-case atom asserted as both fact and query (e.g., \\texttt{is\\_the\\_dog\\_furry.}), with $95/97$ proved samples at $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$. %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s44\nSeed~44 evaded our initial strict classifier (reported $100\\%$ non-hacking); functional cross-validation exposed it---a methodological lesson that text-match classifiers have blind spots and STA's necessity profile is the cross-check.\nWe attribute Prolog's stronger collapse to its simpler operational semantics: a broader gaming basin makes the non-hacking residual disappear---corroborating evidence for Claim 2.\n\n\\subsection{Hacking Taxonomy}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.92\\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_hacking_composition.pdf}\n\\caption{Hacking composition across reward conditions (proved samples; exec-only: seed~42; all other bars: pooled across 3 seeds, per-seed breakdown in Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}). ISR converts H1 into more extreme H7 without reducing total hacking; SFT shows near-zero hacking.}\n\\label{fig:hacking-composition}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\resizebox{\\columnwidth}{!}{%\n\\begin{tabular}{lccccc}\n\\toprule\n\\textbf{Mode} & \\textbf{Exec} & \\textbf{ISR$_{.1}$} & \\textbf{Rnd$_{.1}$} & \\textbf{SFT} & \\textbf{Prolog$_{\\text{ISR}}$} \\\\\n\\midrule\nH1 embed       & 87 & 18 & 71 & 7 & 0$^\\sharp$ \\\\\nH7 duplicate   & 0  & 71 & 14 & 0 & 100$^\\sharp$ \\\\\nH8 prem-subset & 6  & 5  & 8   & 0 & --- \\\\\nTautological   & --- & --- & --- & --- & s42 \\\\\nRepetition     & s43 & --- & --- & --- & --- \\\\\nContradiction  & 1k & --- & --- & --- & --- \\\\\nCmplx.\\ vacuous& 0  & 0  & 4  & 0 & --- \\\\\nQuery-as-fact  & --- & --- & --- & --- & s44 \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\textbf{Non-hacking} & 8  & 6  & 10 & \\textbf{92} & \\textbf{0} \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}}\n\\caption{Eight hacking modes across conditions (\\% of proved samples; exec-only/SFT: seed~42; ISR/Random: pooled across 3 seeds). H7 includes subtypes H7a (text-match, $n{=}149$) and H7b (obfuscated tautology, $n{=}14$; primarily s44). Cells with seed identifiers indicate the mode dominates that seed. $^\\sharp$Prolog row pools strict+functional (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}).}\n\\label{tab:hacking-taxonomy}\n\\end{table}\n\nThe eight modes (Table~\\ref{tab:hacking-taxonomy}; definitions in Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}) span 2 logic types and 6 reward conditions.\nThe three dominant modes---\\textbf{H1} (conclusion embedding, GRPO-universal at $68$--$100\\%$), \\textbf{H7} (conclusion duplication, ISR-amplified ${\\sim}5\\times$; Fisher $p{=}1.3{\\times}10^{-39}$), and \\textbf{H8} (premise-subset gaming; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8})---account for $>90\\%$ of hacking.\nH7 includes an obfuscated subtype \\textbf{H7b} ($14/231$) that evades text-match classifiers via vacuous quantifier wrapping, simultaneously gaming execution reward, ISR, and the hacking detector (\\S\\ref{sec:discussion}).\nThe cross-condition pattern: \\emph{reward formulation determines hacking composition but not prevalence}---all GRPO conditions show $\\geq 80\\%$ pooled hacking; only SFT achieves $92.3\\%$ non-hacking.\nNo GRPO condition produces a high-PR, majority non-hacking run; the taxonomy is descriptive, not exhaustive (query-as-fact gaming was discovered only via functional cross-validation).\n",

    "originalFile": "\\section{Discussion and Conclusion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\nSTA exposes structural degeneration invisible to execution-based evaluation: ISR reveals a 99.5\\% collapse under exec-only GRPO that PR hides, while SFT localizes the failure to RL optimization (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic}).\nRepurposed as a training signal, ISR induces meta-level Goodhart's Law: at $\\beta{=}0.1$, it amplifies H7 tautological gaming ${\\sim}5\\times$ ($70.6\\%$ vs.\\ $13.7\\%$ random, $p{<}10^{-39}$), and the sole apparent ISR gain vanishes ($1.06\\times$) after controlling for premise count; at $\\beta{=}0.3$, each seed converges to a distinct failure mode ($\\text{PR}{=}66{\\pm}42\\%$).\nThe pattern transfers to Prolog (3/3 seeds, 0\\% non-hacking) via distinct mechanisms including query-as-fact gaming that bypasses text-match classifiers (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy}).\nISR semantics are objective-dependent: exec-only \\emph{low} ISR signals redundancy; ISR-rewarded \\emph{high} ISR signals metric gaming.\n\n\\paragraph{Classifier blind spots.}\nH7b (obfuscated tautology) and Prolog query-as-fact gaming demonstrate that text-match classifiers have systematic blind spots exploitable by optimization.\nIn both cases, functional LOO analysis exposed gaming invisible to surface-level detection---H7b via vacuous quantifier wrapping ($10/12$ at $\\text{ISR}{\\geq}0.83$, triple gaming of execution, ISR, and classifier), query-as-fact via $95/97$ tautological samples the strict classifier labeled non-hacking (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}).\nBoth cases argue for mandatory functional cross-validation of text-based hacking classifiers.\n\n\\paragraph{Hacking mode switching.}\nUnder identical reward configurations, different seeds converge to qualitatively different modes (Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}): random $\\beta{=}0.1$ ranges from H8-enriched (s44) to H7-dominant (s43) to highest non-hacking (s42); ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ similarly switches between H1-heavy (s42) and near-pure H7 (s43/s44, ${\\geq}97\\%$).\nSingle-seed results can therefore misrepresent a reward configuration's hacking profile; multi-seed evaluation with per-mode reporting should be standard.\n\nThe asymmetry---reliable observer, unreliable optimizer---may generalize to other perturbation-based diagnostics; ISR and similar metrics should serve as post-hoc evaluation tools, with future work targeting gaming-robust rewards and broader logic types.\n",

    "originalFile": "\\section{Related Work}\n\\label{sec:related-work}\n\n\\paragraph{Neurosymbolic reasoning.}\nLLM-solver pipelines~\\citep{satlm, logic-lm, linc, fover-pei2025, lorp} and RL methods trained on solver feedback~\\citep{mellgren-prolog-grpo, logic-rl, deepseek-prover-v15} evaluate execution accuracy alone, with no diagnostic for structural quality.\nWe use FOLIO~\\citep{folio}, the only public NLI benchmark with both FOL and Prolog annotations.\n\n\\paragraph{Process rewards and reward shaping.}\nLearned PRMs~\\citep{lightman-verify-step, math-shepherd} provide step-level feedback but suffer from reward-model noise and distribution shift~\\citep{processbench}: \\citet{gao-effective-rl-reward} show that auxiliary process/outcome rewards can \\emph{hurt} RL training relative to success-only reward, a finding our ISR-as-reward results corroborate.\nSymbolic feedback approaches~\\citep{rlsf} use solver certificates as token-level rewards, achieving gains on program synthesis; ISR differs in operating at the component level (LOO ablation) rather than the token level, and our results show this structural signal is gamed when optimized.\nRubric-grounded GRPO~\\citep{rubric-grpo} uses structured LLM-judge rewards to improve generalization; ISR is solver-derived and deterministic rather than judge-based, but both represent structured alternatives to scalar execution reward.\nThe shared lesson across these approaches is that reward augmentation introduces proxy risk---our contribution is demonstrating the \\emph{meta}-level variant where the diagnostic itself becomes the target.\n\n\\paragraph{Reward hacking and Goodhart's Law.}\nGoodhart's Law is established theoretically~\\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy, skalse-reward-hacking, karwowski-goodhart-rl} and empirically in RLHF and frontier models~\\citep{gao-reward-overoptimization, bondarenko-spec-gaming, kim-game-formalization}.\n\\citet{kwa-catastrophic-goodhart} show that heavy-tailed reward misspecification causes \\emph{catastrophic} Goodhart where KL regularization provably fails, offering a theoretical lens for why GRPO's KL penalty does not prevent the ISR collapse we observe.\nRecent work further documents spurious reward signals in RLVR~\\citep{spurious-rewards} and reward hacking in frontier models~\\citep{metr-reward-hacking}.\nOur setting differs in three ways: ISR is deterministic and solver-derived rather than learned; gaming \\emph{emerges during RL training} rather than at inference time; and the effect operates at a \\emph{meta} level---the diagnostic for gaming itself becomes gamed when optimized---a phenomenon, to our knowledge, not previously documented.\n\n\\paragraph{Mutation testing.}\nSTA adapts the LOO mutation paradigm~\\citep{demillo1978, jia-harman-survey} to solver inputs; novelty lies in application and findings, not in the perturbation primitive.\n",

    "newString": "### Claim 2: ISR as Reward — First Empirical Evidence of Meta-Level Goodhart ✅ (D033→D043→D051→D057 闭环)\nISR 作为 proxy metric 提供 Manheim & Garrabrant (2019) 框架中 **Goodhart's Law 在 meta 层面的首个经验实例**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，诊断本身成为被 game 的对象。低剂量（β=0.1）2/3 seeds 通过 conclusion duplication 膨胀 ISR；高剂量（β=0.3）产生**极端跨 seed 不稳定 + 多模态 Goodhart 路径**（PR=66±42%，三种不同失败模式：s42 Reward Collapse PR=18%、s43 H1-Dominant Shortcutting PR=86% H1=68.6%、s44 Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart PR=95% H7=100% + ISR=0.742 被同时 game）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。",
    "originalFile": "# Paper Framing — solver-trace-ablation\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (NLP and Symbolic Reasoning track)\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\n\n## Core Claims (3 条 — Goodhart 主线诊断/分析论文)\n\n| Claim | Status | 闭环标志 |\n|-------|--------|----------|\n| Claim 1: STA 诊断能力 | ✅ 已闭环 | 3-seed + 1000-step + hacking taxonomy (8 modes) |\n| Claim 2: Goodhart / ISR 无结构优势 | ✅ 已闭环 (D057) | 3-seed ISR + 3-seed random + premise-count-matched + SFT baseline |\n| Claim 3: 跨逻辑泛化 | ✅ 已闭环 | exec-only 3-seed (D056) + STA 3-seed: **3/3 gaming** — 三种不同 gaming 路径 (H7/degenerate-syntax/query-as-fact) |\n\n### Claim 1: ISR as Reward Hacking Diagnostic ✅\nSolver Trace Ablation (STA) derives ISR (Intensional Structural Reward) via component necessity testing (leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs). ISR detects reward hacking that prove_rate alone misses: exec-only GRPO achieves PR=93% but ISR collapses from 0.6423 (100 steps) to 0.1324 (500 steps) to 0.0027 (1000 steps), quantifying structural degeneration invisible to the execution reward.\n\n**证据**：\n- plan003: 100-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=32%, ISR=0.6423 (proved-only) — early training produces structurally sound formalizations\n- plan001-s42: 500-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=93%, ISR=0.1324, median_ISR=0.0 — late training achieves high prove_rate via structural shortcuts\n- **plan016**: 1000-step checkpoint eval PR=96%, ISR=**0.0027** — ISR 崩塌持续恶化，无回升。94.8% proved 样本 ISR=0。矛盾爆炸式崩塌（components 6.58→18.55）。延长训练是 reward hacking 随时间恶化的干净消融证据\n- ISR 崩塌轨迹完整链：0.6423 (100步) → 0.1324 (500步) → 0.0027 (1000步)\n- 3-seed consistency: s42 redundant embedding (H1=87.1%, ISR=0.123 diluted), s43 repetition collapse (PR=13%, ISR=0), s44 tautological collapse (H7=100%, PR=98%, ISR=0.57 fake-high, n_comp=2.04). All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.\n- Hacking taxonomy: 8 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion duplication with ISR inflation + D085 H7b obfuscated tautology + Prolog query-as-fact gaming)\n\n### Claim 2: ISR as Reward Exhibits Meta-Level Goodhart ✅ (D033→D043→D051→D057 闭环)\nISR 作为 proxy metric 展现 **meta-level Goodhart 效应**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，模型在低剂量 Goodhart ISR 度量本身（β=0.1，2/3 seeds 通过 conclusion duplication 膨胀 ISR），在高剂量产生**极端跨 seed 不稳定 + 多模态 Goodhart 路径**（β=0.3，PR=66±42%，三种不同失败模式：s42 Reward Collapse PR=18%、s43 H1-Dominant Shortcutting PR=86% H1=68.6%、s44 Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart PR=95% H7=100% + ISR=0.742 被同时 game）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。\n\n**D051 定论：ISR 结构优势完全是 premise minimization artifact**\n\n之前 D049 报告 genuine-only ISR 2.4x（ISR 0.601 vs Random 0.252），但 premise-count-matched 分析证明这完全由 ultra-short proofs 驱动：\n\n| 条件 | n | Mean n_prem | Mean ISR |\n|------|---|-------------|----------|\n| ISR s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 11 | ~5.5 | **0.2578** |\n| Random s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 21 | ~6.5 | **0.2434** |\n| **Ratio** | — | — | **1.06×** |\n\n- 控制 premise count 后，ISR vs Random 无差异 (1.06x)\n- ISR s42 的 15/26 \"genuine\" 样本只有 1-2 premises → ISR 天然 ≥0.5（LOO 删 1/2 → 50%+ necessary）\n- ISR s44 的 12 个\"genuine\"全是 n_prem=1 的 **H7b obfuscated tautology**——模型习得 vacuous quantifier wrapping (11/12) + structural simplification (1/12) 策略伪装 tautology，规避文本匹配 H7 检测器。10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8，说明同时 game 了 ISR 评分（meta-Goodhart 直接证据）\n- **Premise minimization 不是推理质量改善，是 ISR metric 的数学性质**：更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高，与证明质量无关\n\n**证据（β=0.1 三 seed + random 对照，D043→D051 定论）**：\n\n| Seed | PR | proved_only ISR | H7 (单premise=结论) | H1 (多premise含结论) | H7+H1 | Genuine % |\n|------|-----|-----------------|---------------------|----------------------|-------|-----------|\n| s42 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.81 | **0.415** | 16.0% (13/81, all H7a) | 51.9% (42/81) | 67.9% | 17.3% (14/81, D078) |\n| s43-r2 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **100%** (61/61, 59 H7a + 2 H7b) | 0% | 100% | 0% |\n| s44 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **100%** (89/89, 77 H7a + 12 H7b, FN corrected) | 0% | 100% | 0% |\n| s42 (random β=0.1) | 0.79 | 0.131 | **0%** (0/79) | **73.4%** (58/79) | 73.4% | **22.8%** (18; H8=4, D086 clean run) |\n| s43-r3 (random β=0.1) | 0.94 | 0.150 | **38.3%** (36/94, all H7a) | **59.6%** (56/94) | 97.9% | **1.1%** (1/94) |\n| s44-r2 (random β=0.1) | 0.89 | 0.070 | **0%** (0/89) | **82.0%** (73/89) | 82.0% | **6.7%** (6/89; H8=16) |\n| **Random 3-seed mean±std** | **0.87±0.08** | **0.117±0.042** | **12.8%±22.1%** | **71.7%±11.4%** | — | **9.5% pooled (25/262)** [6.5%, 13.7%] Wilson 95% CI |\n| **ISR 3-seed mean±std** | **0.77±0.14** | **0.716±0.26** | **70.6% (163/231)** [64.3%, 76.3%] | **18.2%** | — | **6.06% pooled (14/231)** [3.6%, 9.9%] Wilson 95% CI |\n\n**Pooled Genuine Rate 统计检验 (D095 最终)**\n\n| 指标 | ISR | Random | Fisher p (two-sided) | OR |\n|------|-----|--------|---------------------|-----|\n| H7 rate | 163/231=70.6% [64.3%, 76.3%] | 36/262=13.7% [9.9%, 18.6%] | **p = 1.3×10⁻³⁹** | 15.05 |\n| Genuine rate | 14/231=6.06% [3.6%, 9.9%] | 25/262=9.54% [6.5%, 13.7%] | p = 0.182 (n.s.) | 0.612 |\n\nWilson 95% CI 括号内标注。H7 差异极度显著（ISR 放大 H7 频率 ~5x）。Genuine rate 差异不显著（p=0.182），ISR 数值上略低但样本量不足以达到统计显著性。\n\n⚠️ **Pooled H7 解读警告（D095 Reviewer W2）**：pooled 13.7% 由 s43-r3 单 seed 贡献（36/94=38.3%），s42 和 s44-r2 均为 0%。论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 Random H7 的单 seed 来源。不可用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单对比。\n\n⚠️ **H8 审计 + C013 说明**：s44-r2 发现 H1∩H8 重叠（6 个样本同时 H1+H8）。C013 更新公式从 `proved − H7 − H1 − H8` 改为 `proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|`，以 classify_h7.py 集合排除实现为准。s42 完成了完整双侧 H8 审计。s43-r2/s44-r2 为重训新数据，classify_h7.py 直接输出即为准确值。Random pooled H8: 4+1+16=21/262=8.0%。\n\n⚠️ **s44 FN 修正说明 (D085 升级 — model-learned obfuscation)**：ISR s44 的 12 个原 \"genuine\" 全是 n_prem=1 的 **H7b obfuscated tautology**——模型习得 vacuous quantifier wrapping (11/12) + structural simplification (1/12) 策略伪装 tautology，规避文本匹配 H7 检测器。10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8，说明同时 game 了 ISR 评分。这是 **meta-Goodhart 的直接证据**——不仅 reward 被 game，连 tautology 检测器也被 game。H7 子分类：H7a (text-match, 77 个) + H7b (obfuscated, 12 个) = H7 total 89。证据文件：`/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/isr_s44_fn_evidence.json`。\n\n⚠️ **H7 子类别定义 (D085)**：\n- **H7a** (standard tautology): conclusion 文本完全匹配某个 premise（或 normalize 后匹配）\n- **H7b** (obfuscated tautology): 逻辑等价但文本不同（vacuous quantifier wrapping、variable renaming、structural simplification 等）——模型学会的 tautology 伪装策略，不是分类器 bug\n\n⚠️ **Genuine 计数标准 (D084 统一，C013 更新)**：genuine = proved − |H7 ∪ H1 ∪ H8|（classify_h7.py 集合排除实现，H1∩H8 重叠不双减）。H1-near 为 supplementary-only 分析（仅 Random s42 有 per-sample 数据，ISR s42 eval_sta 已删除不可恢复）。两侧对称：ISR s42 genuine=14 (17.3%), Random s42 genuine=18 (22.8%, D086 clean run)。\n\n**关键发现 (D057 3-seed closed)**：\n- **H7 分两层 (D095 Narrative A)**：\n  - **H7a (text-match tautology) 是 GRPO 通用现象**：Random β=0.1 pooled H7=13.7% (36/262), ISR β=0.1 pooled H7=70.6% (163/231), Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹ (OR=15.05)。ISR 信号将 H7 频率放大 ~5x，但 H7a 并非 ISR 独有——Random s43-r3 出现 38.3% H7a (D093)。H7a 结构因条件而异：exec-only = 单前提 P→P；Random = 多前提中嵌入匹配前提。\n  - **H7b (obfuscated tautology) 是 ISR-specific meta-Goodhart**：仅 ISR s44 产出 12 个 H7b（vacuous quantifier wrapping 伪装），Random 3-seed 和 exec-only 3-seed 均为 0 H7b。Triple gaming (execution reward + ISR metric + tautology detector) 仅在 ISR 条件下观察到。\n  - **β 剂量梯度**：Random β=0.1 H7=13.7% → Random β=0.3 H7a=4-7个 → ISR β=0.1 H7=70.6%。ISR 信号是 H7 的主要放大器，additive reward 结构提供基础可能性。\n- **H7b obfuscated tautology 是 meta-Goodhart 新证据线 (D085)**：ISR s44 的 12 个 H7b 样本展示模型不仅 game reward（H7 tautology 获得 r_exec=1），还学会规避 H7 文本匹配检测器（vacuous quantifier wrapping 伪装），且 10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8 同时 game 了 ISR 评分。三层 gaming 堆叠：execution reward + structural metric + diagnostic classifier = meta-Goodhart 的最完整案例\n- **Conclusion embedding 是 GRPO 通用策略**：Random H1=71.7%±11.4% > ISR H1=18.2%。Random 的 H1 率反而更高（ISR 将 H1 转化为 H7）\n- **ISR 改变了 hacking 的组成而非总量**：总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近，但 ISR 将 H1 转化为更极端的 H7\n- **Random PR 更高更稳定**：0.87±0.08 vs ISR 0.77±0.14，ISR-as-reward 无 PR 优势\n- **Premise-count-matched 无差异**：控制 premise 数后 ISR=0.2578 vs Random=0.2434 (1.06x)，ISR 不提供真实结构优势\n- **ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：低剂量 gaming ISR 本身，高剂量 PR 崩塌，premise minimization 不是推理改善\n- **结论**: ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1），作为 reward 不可靠且不提供真实结构优势\n\n**s42 单 seed Hacking Taxonomy 定量对比 (D038→D044→H8 校准)**：\n- ISR β=0.1 genuine: **17.3%** (14/81, D084 标准) vs random β=0.1 genuine: **22.8%** (18/79, D086 clean run) — **-5.5pp（ISR 反而更低）**\n- H1 (multi-premise conclusion embedding, strict): ISR 51.9% vs random 73.4% — GRPO 通用退化\n- H7 (single premise=conclusion, ISR-specific): ISR **16.1%** (H7a 13) vs random **0%** — ISR 特异性\n- H8 (conjunction collapse): ISR **14.8%** (12/81) vs random **5.1%** (4/79, D086 clean run)\n- H3 (complexity-without-utility): ISR **0%** vs random 3.8% — ISR 完全消除此模式\n- 整体 hacking rate (D084 标准, H7+H1_exact+H8): ISR **82.7%** vs random **77.2%** (D086 clean run) — **ISR hacking 更高**\n- ⚠️ s43/s44 均 100% H7（s43: 61/61 incl. 2 H7b; s44: 89/89 incl. 12 H7b），无 H1\n\n**证据（β=0.3 同剂量对照）**：\n\n✅ **D062 数据修正完成**：ISR s43/s44 已用 proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl 重新 eval。s43 genuine 从旧 test_100 的 67% 大幅降至 26.7%（旧数据是假象）。\n\nISR β=0.3 三 seed（✅ 全部 proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl）：\n| Seed | PR | ISR* | H7% | H1% | H8% | Genuine% | 模式 |\n|------|-----|------|-----|-----|-----|----------|------|\n| s42 | **18%** | 0.143 | 0 | 78 | **16.7** (3/18) | **5.6** (1/18, H8 审计后) | **Reward Collapse**（PR 崩塌） |\n| s43 | **86%** | 0.295 | 4.7 | **68.6** | — | **26.7** | **H1-Dominant Shortcutting** |\n| s44 | **95%** | 0.742 | **100** | 0 | — | **0** | **Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart** |\n| mean±std | **66±42** | 0.393±0.31 | 35±55 | 49±42 | — | **10.8±11.5** | 三种不同失败路径 |\n\nRandom β=0.3（✅ 3-seed 全部完成）：\n| Seed | PR | ISR* | H7% | H1% | Genuine% | 状态 |\n|------|-----|------|-----|-----|----------|------|\n| s42 | 48% | 0.094 | **8.3** (4) | 91.7 | 0 | ✅ 完成 (D086: H7a=4) |\n| s43 | 92% | 0.180 (proved-only) | **7.6** (7, all H7a) | 89.1 (82) | 3.26 (3) | ✅ 完成 |\n| s44 | **23%** | **0.000** | 0 | **100** | 0 | ✅ 完成 |\n| 3-seed mean±std | **54.3±35.9%** | 0.087±0.084 | ~6 H7a/seed | ~91% | ~1% | — |\n\n- **D060 叙事调整**：Random β=0.3 也严重崩塌（3-seed mean PR=54.3±35.9%），不再是\"ISR worse than Random\"，而是\"both collapse at high dose, ISR shows more mode diversity\"\n- **D086 β=0.3 H7 gradient evidence (D095 更新)**：Random β=0.3 出现 H7a（s42=4, s43-r4=7），β=0.1 Random H7 为 seed-dependent（s42=0%, s43-r3=38.3%, s44-r2=0%, pooled=13.7%）。H7 梯度：Random β=0.1 (13.7% pooled, seed-dependent) → β=0.3 (4-7 个/seed) → ISR β=0.1 (70.6% pooled)。论文应在 Discussion 讨论此 dose-response gradient\n- ISR β=0.3 三种不同路径 (Reward Collapse / H1-Dominant / Pure H7) vs Random β=0.3 均匀的 H1+ISR=0 崩塌\n- s43 旧数据 (test_100.jsonl) 显示 67% genuine 是假象；正确测试集下 genuine=26.7%，主要靠 H1 hacking（68.6%）\n- **结论**：β=0.3 对两种 reward 条件都是有害的；ISR 特有的是 mode diversity 而非更差的 PR\n\n**β 剂量效应全景**：\n| β | ISR reward PR | Random reward PR | ISR reward ISR | Random reward ISR | 解读 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 0.1 | 0.81 (s42) / 0.61 (s43) / 0.89 (s44) | 0.79 (s42) / 0.94 (s43-r3) / 0.89 (s44-r2) | **0.415** (s42) / **0.830** (s43) / **0.902** (s44) | 0.131 (s42) / 0.150 (s43-r3) / 0.070 (s44-r2) | H7a GRPO-general (Random 13.7%), ISR amplifies 5x (70.6%); H7b ISR-specific; premise-count-matched 无结构差异 |\n| 0.3 | **0.66±0.42** (s42=0.18/s43=0.86/s44=0.95) | **0.54±0.35** (s42=0.48/s43=0.92/s44=0.23) | 0.393±0.31 | 0.087±0.084 (s42=0.094/s43=0.180/s44=0.000) | 多模态 Goodhart 不稳定：Reward Collapse / H1-Dominant Shortcutting / Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart |\n\n**其他 ISR-as-reward 失败模式**：\n- plan005 凸组合 (α·r_exec + β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1): ISR<1 时永远是 penalty → PR 下降 (D010)\n- plan013 convex random: PR=1.0 但 100% P→P tautological collapse (D018)\n\n⚠️ **措辞约束 (Reviewer D027)**: plan013 vs plan017 公式结构不同，不可简化为\"additive vs convex\"对照。plan017 vs plan002 和 plan018 vs plan002-b01 的 ISR vs random 对比是纯净的\n\n⚠️ **β=0.1 三 seed（D043→D051）**：s42 的\"genuine\"优势是 premise minimization artifact。不可报 raw 3-seed mean ISR（被膨胀值污染），不可报 genuine-only 2.4x（premise count artifact）。\n\n**SFT Baseline 对照 (plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4)**：\n- SFT PR=0.14, ISR=0.08, proved-only ISR=0.571\n- H7=0/14 (0%), H1=1/14 (7.1%, folio_167), Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\n\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.\n\n**证据 Part A — Prolog exec-only 3-seed (D056 闭环)**：\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\n  - s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.06%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6.1% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 82%, H8 18%, genuine 6.7% | control |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.143 | PR 崩塌（经典 Goodhart） | post-Goodhart |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |\n| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | H7a=8.3% (4), H1=91.7%, genuine=0% | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.92 | 0.166 | H7a=7 (7.6%), H1=82 (89.1%), genuine=3 (3.3%) | control |\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **\"Single-logic risk\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~6%).\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\n13. **\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \"Genuine=100%\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\n\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\n\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (100%, H7a=59 + H7b=2), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 \"hack mode switching\" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。\n\n### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)\n- 不用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单 pooled 对比\n- 正确 framing: \"H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。\"\n- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 each seed converges to a distinct pathway (Appendix~\\ref{app:detailed-results}). %% registry: plan002-additive-b03-s42/s43/s44\nSeed~42: \\emph{reward collapse} ($\\text{PR}{=}18\\%$), following the pattern described by~\\citet{gao-reward-overoptimization}.\nSeed~43: \\emph{H1 shortcutting} ($\\text{PR}{=}86\\%$, $\\text{H1}{=}69\\%$, only $27\\%$ non-hacking).\nSeed~44: \\emph{pure H7 meta-Goodhart} ($\\text{PR}{=}95\\%$, $\\text{H7}{=}100\\%$, $\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}0.742$, $0\\%$ non-hacking)---simultaneously gaming \\emph{both} execution and structural metrics through the same construction.\nThe random control at $\\beta{=}0.3$ is equally unstable ($\\text{PR}{=}54{\\pm}35\\%$, spanning 23--92\\%) with near-universal H1 hacking ($91\\%$, pooled) and ${\\leq}4\\%$ non-hacking per seed; high-dose proxy weighting destabilizes training regardless of signal source; ISR produces diverse failure modes (reward collapse, H1 shortcutting, H7 meta-Goodhart) while random converges to H1-dominated hacking with sporadic H7 ($7\\%$ pooled, $0$--$7$ H7a per seed) (Appendix~\\ref{app:beta03}).\n\n\\subsection{Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer}\n\\label{sec:crosslogic}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.95\\columnwidth]{figures/fig4_prolog_comparison.pdf}\n\\caption{Cross-logic results. \\textbf{Left}: FOL vs.\\ Prolog exec-only $\\text{ISR}^*$ across 3 seeds (s44 Prolog $0.387$ is a small-sample artifact, $n{=}21$). \\textbf{Right}: Prolog ISR-reward gaming-mode composition---all 3 seeds reach 0\\% non-hacking through distinct mechanisms.}\n\\label{fig:prolog-comparison}\n\\end{figure}\n\nWe replicate the FOL pipeline with Prolog/SWI-Prolog: (i) does ISR retain diagnostic value on a structurally different logic; ISR-rewarded \\emph{high} ISR signals \\emph{metric gaming} (\\S\\ref{sec:taxonomy-method})---the same number does not license the same reasoning-quality conclusion.\n\n\\textbf{Prolog ISR-as-reward: universal Goodhart.}\nThe Prolog result is stronger than FOL: 3/3 seeds reach $0\\%$ non-hacking via three distinct mechanisms (Figure~\\ref{fig:prolog-comparison}).\nSeed~42: clean H7 tautologies ($\\text{ISR}^{*}{=}1.0$, all $50/100$ proved). %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s42\nSeed~43: \\emph{degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog} (e.g., \\texttt{MARY\\_IS\\_AN\\_AVA\\-DEOTE.}); strict text-match returns $\\text{H7}{=}0$ but functional analysis shows all 38 proved samples have $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$ (functionally H7). %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s43\nSeed~44: \\emph{query-as-fact gaming}---the model transforms the NL question into a snake-case atom asserted as both fact and query (e.g., \\texttt{is\\_the\\_dog\\_furry.}), with $95/97$ proved samples at $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$. %% registry: plan006-prolog-sta-s44\nSeed~44 evaded our initial strict classifier (reported $100\\%$ non-hacking); functional cross-validation exposed it---a methodological lesson that text-match classifiers have blind spots and STA's necessity profile is the cross-check.\nWe attribute Prolog's stronger collapse to its simpler operational semantics: a broader gaming basin makes the non-hacking residual disappear---corroborating evidence for Claim 2.\n\n\\subsection{Hacking Taxonomy}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=0.92\\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_hacking_composition.pdf}\n\\caption{Hacking composition across reward conditions (proved samples; exec-only: seed~42; all other bars: pooled across 3 seeds, per-seed breakdown in Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:fol-detailed}). ISR converts H1 into more extreme H7 without reducing total hacking; SFT shows near-zero hacking.}\n\\label{fig:hacking-composition}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\resizebox{\\columnwidth}{!}{%\n\\begin{tabular}{lccccc}\n\\toprule\n\\textbf{Mode} & \\textbf{Exec} & \\textbf{ISR$_{.1}$} & \\textbf{Rnd$_{.1}$} & \\textbf{SFT} & \\textbf{Prolog$_{\\text{ISR}}$} \\\\\n\\midrule\nH1 embed       & 87 & 18 & 71 & 7 & 0$^\\sharp$ \\\\\nH7 duplicate   & 0  & 71 & 14 & 0 & 100$^\\sharp$ \\\\\nH8 prem-subset & 6  & 5  & 8   & 0 & --- \\\\\nTautological   & --- & --- & --- & --- & s42 \\\\\nRepetition     & s43 & --- & --- & --- & --- \\\\\nContradiction  & 1k & --- & --- & --- & --- \\\\\nCmplx.\\ vacuous& 0  & 0  & 4  & 0 & --- \\\\\nQuery-as-fact  & --- & --- & --- & --- & s44 \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\textbf{Non-hacking} & 8  & 6  & 10 & \\textbf{92} & \\textbf{0} \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}}\n\\caption{Eight hacking modes across conditions (\\% of proved samples; exec-only/SFT: seed~42; ISR/Random: pooled across 3 seeds). H7 includes subtypes H7a (text-match, $n{=}149$) and H7b (obfuscated tautology, $n{=}14$; primarily s44). Cells with seed identifiers indicate the mode dominates that seed. $^\\sharp$Prolog row pools strict+functional (\\S\\ref{sec:crosslogic}).}\n\\label{tab:hacking-taxonomy}\n\\end{table}\n\nThe eight modes (Table~\\ref{tab:hacking-taxonomy}; definitions in Appendix~\\ref{app:hacking-modes}) span 2 logic types and 6 reward conditions.\nThe three dominant modes---\\textbf{H1} (conclusion embedding, GRPO-universal at $68$--$100\\%$), \\textbf{H7} (conclusion duplication, ISR-amplified ${\\sim}5\\times$; Fisher $p{=}1.3{\\times}10^{-39}$), and \\textbf{H8} (premise-subset gaming; Appendix~\\ref{app:h8})---account for $>90\\%$ of hacking.\nH7 includes an obfuscated subtype \\textbf{H7b} ($14/231$) that evades text-match classifiers via vacuous quantifier wrapping, simultaneously gaming execution reward, ISR, and the hacking detector (\\S\\ref{sec:discussion}).\nThe cross-condition pattern: \\emph{reward formulation determines hacking composition but not prevalence}---all GRPO conditions show $\\geq 80\\%$ pooled hacking; only SFT achieves $92.3\\%$ non-hacking.\nNo GRPO condition produces a high-PR, majority non-hacking run; the taxonomy is descriptive, not exhaustive (query-as-fact gaming was discovered only via functional cross-validation).\n",
